		muṉṉiṟ kaṭalkaḷuḷ mūḻkuvitta ceṉṉi 	- irācarāca tēvarulā
araṇmaṉikaḷil aracaṉ taṅkum taṉi māḷikiyum poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. cuntarac cōḻaṉip poṉ māḷikit tuñciya  tēvar eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
koṅkunāṭṭiṟ kiṭitta poṉ koṅkuppoṉ eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum curaṅkattiṟ  poṉṉeṭukkum kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) paṇṭik koṅkunāṭṭic cērntatē.
		koḻuṅkoṭi yaṟukiyuṅ kuvaḷiyuṅ kalantu
		viḷaṅkukatirt  toṭutta viriyal cūṭṭip
		pāruṭip paṉarpōṟ paḻicciṉar kitoḻa
		ēroṭu niṉṟōr ērmaṅ kalamum 	(cilap.10:13:2-5)
eṉṉum cilappatikāra nāṭukāṇ kāti yaṭikaṭku, cenneṟ kati rōṭē aṟukiyum  kuvaḷiyiyum  kalantu  toṭutta māliyi   mēḻiyilē  cūṭṭip pāri iraṇṭākap  piḷappārip  pōlap pōṟṟuvār toḻap poṉṉēr pūṭṭi niṉṟōr ērip pāṭum ēr maṅkalap pāṭṭumeṉka eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār uri varin tuḷḷār.  
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akaramutali, itip piṉpaṟṟip poṉṉēr eṉpataṟku, paruva kālattil nalla nāḷil mutaṉ muṟiyāka uḻuṅ kalappi eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
paṇṭi makatanāṭṭūrkaḷil āṇṭutōṟum mutaluḻavu uḻumpōtu, ūrttalivaṉ poṉṉāṟ ceyta ērip pūṭṭi uḻavar variciyil mutalil niṉṟu, piṟar piṉvara, oru paṭiccā lōṭṭit toṭaṅkivippāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatāl, kumarināṭṭil ataṟku mūlamāṉa vaḻakkam iruntirukkalāmeṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
uruvap paḵṟē riḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi poṟṟēri yuṭiya vaṉāyiruntāṉ.
		polantērmicip polivutōṉṟi
		mākkaṭal nivanteḻutaruñ
		ceññāyiṟṟuk kaviṉimātō	(puṟam. 4)
eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭutal kāṇka. paṇṭit tamiḻaracariṉ tēr kutiri yāṉikaḷ poṟpaṭikaḷāl aṇiceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa.
		makiḻā tītta iḻiyaṇi neṭuntēr	(puṟam.123:4)
		pāṭip peṟṟa poṉṉaṇi yāṉi	(puṟam.177 :3)
		valampaṭu tāṉi vēntar
		polampaṭik kalimā eṇṇu vōrē	(puṟam. 116)
aracaruṭiya pōr muracum atupōṉṟa piṟa ciṉṉaṅkaḷum poṉṉāl aṇiceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
		polaṅkuḻi yuḻiñiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
		kuruti vēṭki yurukeḻu muracam	(puṟam. 5)
ki.mu. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil yūtēyā nāṭṭiyāṇṭa cālōmōṉ aracaṉ (1015-975), oppīr (Ophir) eṉṉum intiyat tuṟimukattiṉiṉṟu   varuvitta poruḷkaḷuḷ poṉṉum oṉṟu. at tuṟinakar, vēmpāy eṉappaṭum pampāy mānakarkku vaṭakkē 37 kal tolivilirunta, upparā allatu upparakā eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarāṟ karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār atu orukāl pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkari yiluḷḷa uvariyā yirukkalāmeṉṟu karutiṉar. etuvāyiṉum, oppīr ōr intiyat tuṟinakar eṉpatu uṟuti. cālōmōṉ kālattiṟ cēranāṭu kuccaram (kucarāttu) vari paraviyiruntatu.
toṉṟutoṭṭup poṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāka ēṟṟumatiyāki vantataṉāl, cēkkacuppiyar (Shakespeare) tam paṉṉiraṇṭām iravu' (Twelfth Night) eṉṉum nāṭakattil, poṉṉi intiya khiH‘(metal of India) eṉṟār (2:5).
(4) kumarināṭṭil poṉ kiṭittamipaṟṟiya neṭṭimiyār kūṟṟu:
	..............................................j§nfh¢
		cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
		munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
		naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē	(puṟam. 9)
itaṉ paḻiya vuri: tammuṭiya kōvākiya civanta nīrmiyi yuṭiya pōkkaṟṟa paciya poṉṉik kūttarkku vaḻaṅkiya munnīrk kaṭaṟṟeyvattiṟ keṭutta viḻāviṉi yuṭiya neṭiyōṉā luḷatākkappaṭṭa nalla nīriyuṭiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṉ maṇaliṉum palakālam''.
kumarināṭṭu māntar mutaṟkālattil iyaṟki nīrnili
kaḷiyē cārntiruntataṉālum, āṟillāta viṭamellām aṭarn
tiruṇṭa kāṭā yiruntataṉālum, makkaḷtoki mikamikap perumpālum āṟukaḷi yaṭuttē paraviyiruttal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, perumali nāṭāṉa kuṟiñciyiyum, kuṟumali nāṭāṉa mulliyiyuṅ kaṭantapiṉ, karaṭum kallum murampum pāṟiyum aruki nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum mikka marutanilattiṟkē iyaṟki
yākap paraviyirun tiruppar.
aṅku nellum vāḻiyum pōṉṟa nilittiṇi (tāvara) vakikaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟa meṉṉilattiyuṅ kaṇṭu, avaṟṟic ceyaṟkiyāka viḷivikka muṉintu uḻittaṉar. eṉṟum nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷik karical nilattilum, iṭiyiṭṭu nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷic cival nilattilum payiriṭṭaṉar. ceṭi koṭi puṟkaḷital, kalleṭuttal, uramiṭutal, paṉmuṟiyuḻutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (palliyāṭutal), puḻuti yākkutal, kāyaviṭutal eṉṉum palvakiyil tiruttappaṭṭa nilam cey eṉappaṭṭatu. ceytal = tiruttutal. pēraḷavākat tiruttappaṭṭatu naṉcey eṉṟum, ciṟṟaḷavākat tiruttappaṭṭatu puṉcey eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa. naṉcey uram mikutiyāka vikkappaṭuvatāl vayal eṉṟum (vi-(vay)-vayal), cēṟupaṭutalāl ceṟu eṉṟum, paḷḷamāyiruttalāl paṇṇi yeṉṟum, pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷañ cērntiruppiṉ kaḻaṉi yeṉṟum, nīṇṭa kālam paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭup paḻamiyāṉapiṉ paḻaṉam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟatu. nelvayalil uḻumpōtum aṟuvaṭi ceyyum pōtum kiṇippaṟi (uṟumi) koṭṭappaṭṭatu.
nellum vāḻiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi naṉceyilum, cōḷamum kēḻvarakum pōṉṟavaṟṟip puṉceyilum, varakum cāmiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi vāṉāvārik kolliyilum  payiriṭṭaṉar. nīrppācaṉa ēntu (vacati) irukkumiṭa mellām puṉceyi naṉceyākkiṉar. patiṉeṇ kūlamum eḷḷum iñciyum mañcaḷum paruttiyum piṟavum viḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
nīrppācaṉattiṟku mutalil āṟum poykiyum ākiya iyaṟki nīrnilikaḷiyē cārntiruntaṉar. piṉṉar avi yillāta viṭaṅkaḷil, kaṇṇāṟuṅ kālvāyum ēriyuṅ kuḷamum veṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. avi ellārkkum potuvā yiruntamiyālum, kōṭikkālattil vaṟṟippōṉamiyālum, āṇṭu muḻutum payaṉpaṭumāṟum contavuṭimiyā yirukkumāṟum, nīr curakkumiṭamellām kiṇaṟukaḷiyum veṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar. akkālattil makkaḷtoki mikak kuṟivāyiruntamiyālum, nilapparappil mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēlum maramaṭarnta aṭaviyā yiruntamiyālum, kōṭimaḻi, kālamaḻi, aṭimaḻi, paṭimaḻi ākiya nālvaki maḻiyum kālan  tappātu peytu vantaṉa.  a(l)kāla vampa maḻiyum orōvoru camiyam peytatu.
kālvāy nīriṟikka iṟipeṭṭi (iṟikūṭi), uḻaṇi (ōṇi) mutaliya karuvikaḷum, kiṇaṟṟu nīriṟikka ēṟṟam, kammāli eṉṉum poṟikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. am=nīr. am-kam=nīr. ālutal=āṭutal, cuṟṟutal. āl-āli=cuṟṟivarum poṟi. cekkāli, karumpāli mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka. iṉṟum, tirukkōvalūrkkum villipurattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ūrkaḷiṟ cuṟṟuk kavaliyāṭutal kāṇka. kammāli-kamali-kavali. ēṟṟam kiyāl iyakkappaṭuvatu. atu kittulā, āḷēṟun  tulā eṉa iruvaki. kammāli erutu pūṭṭi iyakkappaṭuvatu.
neṟcōṟu marutanilattil mutaṉ mutalākap poṅkappaṭṭatu. col = nel. col - coṉṟi - cōṟu. ciṟṟuṇṭiyākap payaṉ paṭumāṟu, aricippori porikkavum avaliṭikkavum kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. payaṟu vakikaḷi avittum cuṇṭiyum tiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟiṉ paruppāṟ kummāyamuṅ kūṭṭum cāṟuṅ kuḻampum ākkic cōṟṟuṭaṉ kalantuṇṭaṉar. vāḻi, vaḻutuṇi(kattari), veṇṭi, curi, pīrkku, pūcaṇi mutaliya kāykaḷit taṉittum paruppuṭaṉ cērttum kaṟivakikaḷākac camittaṉar.
ūṉukku, vayalilum vāykkālilum piṭitta mīṉiyum āmiyiyum, vīṭṭil vaḷartta āṭu kōḻikaḷiyum payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. iṭiyiṭi mullikkuṅ kuṟiñcikkuñ ceṉṟu, tamakku vēṇṭiya vilaṅku paṟavikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiyum vantaṉar.
kārattiṟku miḷakiyum, iḷaṅkārattiṟkum cuvikkum veṅkāyattiyum, niṟattiṟkum maṇattiṟkum caḷimuṟippiṟkum mañcaḷiyum, cappeṉṟiruttali nīkkic cuviyūṭṭaṟku umaṇ eṉṉum uvarnilattu uppiyum camiyaliṟ cērttukkoṇṭaṉar.
kuṟiñciyilum mulliyilum perumpālum pālum cūṭ                  ṭiṟicciyumē uṇṭataṉāl, av vuṇaviṟku uvarppupporuḷ tēvi yillātiruntatu. mulli nilattil, tiṉi varaku cāmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ariciyip pāliluñ camittirukkalām. maruta nilattiṟku vanta piṉpē ciṟanta muṟiyiṟ camiyal toṭaṅkiyatāl, poṅkal puḻuṅkal aviyal cuṇṭal tuvaṭṭal puraṭṭal kāyccal poriyal mutaliya viṉikaḷi nīriṟ ceytaṟku, uvarppuc carakkuc cērkkavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. uppu viḷikkum uvarnīrk kaṭalic  cārnta neytal  nilatti aṭukkumuṉ, umaṇiyē payaṉpaṭutti  yiruttal vēṇṭum. umaṇ eṉpatu uvarmaṇ. ati mutalil viṟṟavar, piṉpu neytal nilattu uppaḷattil uppu viḷitta pōtum, umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
		kaḷarnilat tuppiṟanta vuppiṉic cāṉṟōr
		viḷinilattu  nelliṉ viḻumitāk koḷvar	(nālaṭi.133)
poriyal mutalil āviṉ neyyilēyē ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉālēyē, piṉṉar eḷḷilirun teṭukkappaṭṭa neyp poruḷ eṇṇey (eḷney) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. eḷḷiṉ neyyē mutaṟ kālattil peruvaḻakkamāka vaḻaṅkiṉataṉāl, piṟkālattil, āviṉ ney tavira maṟṟellā neyvakikaṭkum eṇṇey eṉpatu potuppeya rāyiṟṟu.
camiyaṟku vēṇṭiya neruppu, nāḷtoṟum ñelikōlāl virintu kaṭiyappaṭṭatu. atu vīṭutoṟum iṟavāṇattiṟ cerukappaṭṭiruntatu. kuṟiñci vāṇarum mulli vāṇarum vēṭṭikkārarum, ati eṉṟuṅ kiyil vittiruntaṉar.
		illiṟic cerīiya ñelikōl pōla	(puṟam. 315:4)
		kāṉavar pottiya ñelitī viḷakkattu	(puṟam. 247:2)
		pulleṉ mālic ciṟutī ñeliyuṅ
		kallā viṭiyaṉ pōla	(puṟam.331:4)
		celvat tōṉṟalōr valvil vēṭṭuvaṉ
		toḻutaṉa ṉeḻuvēṟ kikavit tirīi
		iḻuti ṉaṉṉa vāṉiṇak koḻuṅkuṟi
		kāṉatar mayaṅkiya iḷiyar vallē
		tāmvan teytā vaḷavi yoyyeṉat
		tāṉñeli tīyiṉ virivaṉaṉ cuṭṭuniṉ
		irumpē rokkaloṭu tiṉmeṉat tarutaliṉ	(puṟam.150:7-13)
ivi piṟkālattuc ceyyuḷāyiṉum, muṟkālattu nilimi yiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tīppaṭṭa viṭattil kaḷimaṇ iṟukik kaṟpōṟ keṭṭiyāṉa tik kaṇṭu, kaḷimaṇṇāṟ pāṉi caṭṭikaḷi vaṉintu cuṭṭuc camiyaṟ kalaṅkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
paruttip pañcāl nūliḻittup palvaki āṭikaḷi neytukoṇṭaṉar. nūl nūṟṟal peṇṭirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpati, nuṇṇiya palavāya pañcunuṉikaḷāṟ kivaṉ makaṭūu taṉatu ceyki māṇpiṉāl ōriḻip  paṭuttalām, ulakattu nūṉūṟṟa leṉpatu"  eṉṉum iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷurik kūṟṟālum(nūṟpā.1), 
		pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva liḻiyākac
		ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
		kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
		miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu	(naṉ. 23)
eṉṉum  naṉṉūṟ  potuppāyira  nūṟpāvālum  aṟiyalām. vīṭṭiliruntu   ceyyum  toḻilukku  ēṟṟavar  peṇṭirē. parutti nūl nūṟṟa peṇṭir paruttip peṇṭir eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
	paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuva laṉṉa	(puṟam. 125)
kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭiya peṇ vīṭṭuvēliyuñ ceyyalām; naṉcey puṉceyk kāṭṭuvēliyuñ ceyyalām. kaṇavaṉi yiḻanta kimpeṇ, taṉ kaṟpik kāttukkoṇṭu piḻikka vīṭṭuvēliyē ceytal kūṭum. attakiya vēli akkālattu nūl nūṟṟalē. ātalāṟ kimpeṇṭir ataṉi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.
		āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta
		nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval pōla	(naṟ.353)
āṭinecavu irupālārāluñ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu.
aḻakiṟkum maṇattiṟkum, palvaki naṟumalarkaḷik kaṇṇi yākak kaṭṭiyum māliyākat toṭuttum, āṭavar kaḻuttilum peṇṭir koṇṭiyilum nāḷtoṟum putitu putitāka aṇintu koṇṭaṉar.
marutanila maṇ keṭṭiyā yiruntataṉāl, uyarnta maṇcuvar eḻuppip peruvīṭu kaṭṭik kūri vēyntukoṇṭaṉar. ūrt talivarāyiruntavar, mulli nilattuk karaṭukaḷiluṅ kuṉṟukaḷilu miruntu kaṟkoṇarvittu, kāri (maṇal kalanta cuṇṇāmpuc cāntu) pūcik kalvīṭu kaṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar. cuṭumaṭkalan tōṉṟiyapiṉ cuṭumaṭ kalluñ cuṭumaṇ ōṭun tōṉṟiyataṉāl, atikāramuñ celvamuñ ciṟantavar kāripūcic ceṅkal maṉikaḷ kaṭṭi ōṭu vēyntukoṇṭaṉar. ōṭu vēyātu maṭṭamāka mukaṭa mitta kārivīṭu maccuvīṭeṉappaṭṭatu. cuṭumaṇ eṉpatu, mutalil  cuṭṭa kalam, cuṭṭa ceṅkal, cuṭṭa ōṭu ākiya mūṉṟiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
cuṭumaṇ =  1. maṭkalam (cilap. 14: 146 arum.). 2. ceṅkal .cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunakar varippil (perumpāṇ.405). 3. ōṭu. cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ (cilap. 14:146).
cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭumaṭ palaki yeṉṟuñ collappaṭṭatu.
	payiluñ cuṭumaṭ palakipala koṇarvittu	(periyapu.ēyarkōṉ. 49)
iṟutiyil ceṅkal eṉṉum peyarē nilittatu. pārvikku naṉṟāyirukkavum vaṇṇa ōviyam variyavum, ceṅkaṟ cuvarellām maṇal kalavāta veṇcāntiṉāl tīṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇcāntu pūciya kārivīṭu, kūri vēynta maṇ vīṭṭoṭu oppunōkkiya pōtu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nakar eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. nakutal =  viḷaṅkutal. naku-nakar. nakarmikka vūrum ciṉiyāku peyarāka nakar eṉappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭik kuṟikkum kuṭi eṉṉuñ collum, kuṭimikka vūrik kuṟittal kāṇka. (e-ṭu: maṉṉārkuṭi, kārikkuṭi). im mayakkan tavirttu ūriyē kuṟikka nakari eṉṉuñ col eḻuntatu. nakarkaḷi (kāri vīṭukaḷi) uṭiyatu nakari. periya nakar (ūr) nakaram eṉappaṭṭatu. ikkālat til mānakar eṉpar. aṅṅaṉañ collat tēviyilli. am' eṉpatu perumip poruḷ kuṟikkum piṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu: nili-niliyam, viḷakku-viḷakkam, mati-matiyam = muḻunilā.
nakarkaḷilēyē nākarikan tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟē tirintamintatu. nakar-nakarakam-nakarikam-nākarikam. itaṉ viḷakkatti eṉ paṇṭit tamiḻ nākarikamum paṇpāṭum'  eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.
aracaṉ allatu avaṉuṭiya tuṇiyatikārikaḷ vatital, aṟiñarum pulavarum pērāciriyarum vāḻtal,  kaṇakkāyar paḷḷi kaḷum taṉippaṭṭa perumpulavariṉ uyarnilik kalvi niliyaṅkaḷum amintiruttal, perumpālār eḻutap paṭikkat terintavarā yiruntu tiruttamākap pēcutal, ellārun tiruntiya paḻakkavaḻak kaṅkaḷi mēṟkoḷḷutal, aḻakiya āṭiyaṇikaḷ uṭal maṟiya aṇiyap
paṭutal, tuppuravāṉa kārivīṭukaḷum māṭamāḷikikaḷum kūṭa
kōpuraṅkaḷum nērāṉa perunterukkaḷum puticālakaṅkaḷum iruttal, ēntāṉa aḻakiya vūrtikaḷ iyaṅkutal, uyarnta aṟucuvi yuṇṭikaḷ uṇṇappaṭutal, palavaki yuyarnta piṟanāṭṭu aimpula nukarcci yarumporuṭ kaṭikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttiruttal, kaḷavuṅ koḷḷiyum nikaḻāvāṟu ūrkāvalar allum pakaluṅ kāttal, koliyāḷikaḷiyum piṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiyun taṇṭikkum aṟamaṉṟamum, tirukkōyilkaḷum tuṟaviyar maṭamum coṟpoḻi vuk kūṭamum paṭṭimaṇṭapamum uṇmi  mutaliya ētukkaḷāl, nakaraṅkaḷum nakarkaḷumē nākarikap piṟappiṭamāyiṉa. anākarikaṉi nāṭṭup puṟattāṉeṉṟum, paṭṭikkāṭṭāṉeṉṟum colvatē, iti valiyuṟuttum. nakara mēmpāṭukaḷ nakarkaḷiṟ kuṉṟiyirukku mēṉum nākarikattiṟ kētuvāṉaviyē. nākarikam nakariṟ piṟantateṉiṉ, nakarattiṟ ciṟanta teṉalām. pāṇṭiyaṉ maturi oru nakaram (mānakar). akkālattu nelli (tirunelvēli) oru nakar. viki maturi pōṉṟatē paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟut teṉmaturi.
nākarikattiṉ mūḷi kuṟiñcinilattilēyē tōṉṟiyatēṉum, atu muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintatu  marutanilattu nakarppāṅkē yeṉṟum, kuṟiñci nākarikamum mulli nākarikamum maruta nākarikattiṉ kīḻnilikaḷē yeṉṟum, neytalnili vaṇika vaḷarccikkum celvap perukkattiṟkumē ētuveṉṟum, pālinili nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉṟip paṇpāṭṭiḻipiyē kāṭṭumeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
marutanilak kumukāya vāḻvil, orumaṉi maṇamum iṟappu
vari piriyā ilvāḻvum perumpāṉmiyāyiṉa. oruvaṉāl maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭamikku aṟikuṟiyākap peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttiṟ poṟṟāli kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. paṉmaṉi maṇamum tīrvi muṟiyum toṭarntaṉa vēṉum, tolli mikutiyum paṇpāṭṭiḻipumpaṟṟi avi tāḻvākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ūrt talivaṉum avaṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa aracarum, iṉpac ciṟappu nōkkip paṉmaṉi maṇattiyē kaṭip piṭittārēṉum,ataṉ iḻivi avaratu teyvattaṉmiyāṉa atikāram muṟṟilum maṟittuviṭṭatu. marutanilattu niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppum payirttoḻiliṟ kuṭumpak kūṭṭuḻippum, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kātali vaḷarttu maṇa vāḻkkiyi nīṭikkac ceytaṉa. uyiruk kiṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittataṉāl, vayiṟāravuṇṭu iṉ puṟavum, uḻikka iyalātavark kutavi aṟam vaḷarkkavum ētuvāyiṟṟu.
poṟkāla  marutanila  āṭcittuṟiyil, taṉiyūrāṭci pōy, oru pērūrum ataṉic cūḻnta uṭkiṭi pōṉṟa ciṟṟūrkaḷuñ cērnta kūṭṭūrāṭci  tōṉṟi, piṉṉar ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarāṭci ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
taṉiyūrāṭci eṉpatu, elliyaḷavil ikkālattu nāṭ ṭāṇmikkāraṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, mūppaṉ, talivaṉ, kavuṇṭaṉ, kuṭumpaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum kuṭittalivaṉ āṭci pōṉṟatu. āyiṉ, atikāra aḷavil piṟkālattu vēntaṉ āṭci pōṉṟē kōṉmi (Sovereignty) koṇṭatu. kūṭṭūrāṭci eṉpatu, kirēkka nāṭṭu nakara nāṭu (City State) pōṉṟatu. ātalāl, atuvuṅ kōṉmi koṇṭatē. kuṟunila maṉṉarāṭci eṉpatu, pāri  kāri mutaliya vēḷirāṭci pōṉṟatu; ciṉṉūṟūrkaḷum paṉṉūṟūrkaḷuṅ koṇṭatu. am maṉṉariṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkika ḷellām nakarkaḷ. amiccarum paṭimaṟavarum avarukkut tuṇiyā
yiruntaṉar.
marutanilak kuṭiyiruppup pōṉṟē, mulli kuṟiñcinilak kuṭiyiruppukaḷum āṭciyil māṟutalaṭintaṉa. atāvatu, taṉiyūrāṭci pōyk kūṭṭūrāṭci tōṉṟiṟṟu. paṭittuṇi yiṉmiyāṟ kuṟunila vāṭci tōṉṟavilli. iv vāṭci māṟṟam paṇṭamāṟṟu, viḻāk kāṇṭal, vēṭṭiyāṭal mutaliya toṭarpukaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, marutampōl nākarika vaḷarcci yaṭiyavilli. itaṟku nila amippē aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam. ataṉāl, nakaraṅkaḷun tōṉṟavilli; ūrp peyarkaḷum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. marutanilattu ūrē ūreṉappaṭṭatu. ur=uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal. ur-ūr. uḻavuttoḻilē nilattoṭu porunti niliyāka vāḻa ētuvāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, ciṟṟūr  pērūrum, pērūr mūtūrum āyiṉa. nakaraṅkaḷ perumipaṟṟip pērūr eṉṟum, mutumipaṟṟi mūtūr eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
		vāṇaṉ pērūr	(maṇimē.3 :123)
		atirāc ciṟappiṉ maturi mūtūr	(cilap.pati.39)
mullinilattiṟ kālnaṭikaṭkup pulveḷi tēṭi aṭikkaṭi iṭam peyaravēṇṭi yiruntatu. ataṉāṟ kaṭṭi maṇmēṟ kūri vēyntu, uyaramiṉṟit tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷik kaṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl an nilattūr pāṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭuttal=tāḻtal, tāḻvāyiruttal. paṭu-pāṭu-pāṭi. iṉi, paṭu-paṭi-pāṭi eṉṟumām. vīṭukaḷ ceṟintiruntataṉāl, av vūr cēri eṉavum paṭṭatu.
kuṟiñcinilak kuṭiyiruppu nilampaṟṟik kuṟicci yeṉappaṭṭatu. kuṟiñci-kuṟicci. ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭamāka vāḻntataṉāl ciṟukuṭi yeṉavum paṭṭatu. kuṭiyeṉṟatu kuṭit tokutiyi. kuṭi =  vaṭṭa māṉa ciṟu vīṭu.
poṟkāla moḻi, pakucoṉṉili (Inflexional Stage) kaṭantu tokunili (Synthetic Stage), paltokunili (Polysynthetic Stage) eṉṟa nilikaḷum aṭintiruttal vēṇṭum. 
muṟiyeṇ         nili 		eṭuttukkāṭṭu               
1.	acinili:
           	(1) iyalnili	ē, uḷ, pal
	(2) tirinili	yā, uṇ (uṭkoḷ).
          		(par - paru - peru).

		nallatu.

		cuḷ - cuṇ + am + pu  =  				cuṇṇampu - cuṇṇāmpu.
4.	pakucoṉṉili  	(perumakaṉ-) pemmāṉ,

         		ē + um = ēm - yām.

 aritu - arutu = kūṭātu. 	ceyyarutu = ceyyakkūṭātu.
putuk kaṟkālattil, peyarviṉi yeṉṉum col vēṟu
pāṭum, taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki yeṉṉum iṭavēṟupāṭum, orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum eṇ vēṟupāṭumē iruntiruntu, poṟkālattil irutiṇiyum aimpālum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. marutanilac coṟkaḷāl colvaḷam perukiṟṟu.
mataviyal vāḻkkiyil, poṟkāla marutanilat tamiḻar, paḻiya kuṟiñci mullit teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṟanta aracaṉiyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
akkālattil, perumpaṇṇikaḷi lellām, tāmāka vantu oṭṭikkoṇṭavarum vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭavarumāka, irucār aṭimiyar iruntaṉar. avar tappi ōṭippōṉālum aṭimiya reṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvataṟku, avar kātil tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
teyvappaṟṟu mikunta piṟkālattil, āṇṭi mutal aracaṉvari ellā vakuppārum tammi iṟiyaṭiyār eṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟkut tam kātu kuttit tuḷiyiṭṭukkoṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. at tuḷi tūrntu pōkāvāṟē, mutalil ōlic curuḷum piṉṉarp poṉṉōliyum piṟavaṇikaḷum peṇṭir aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāmpaṭam (nākapaṭam), taṇṭoṭṭi, aricittaḻuppu, pūccikkūṭu, mēlīṭu eṉṉum aivaki nakikaḷi yaṇitaṟkut tam iḷamiyilēyē kuṇukku eṉṉum kutampiyāl tam kātut tuḷiyip peritāka vaḷarttuk koṇṭaṉar. kumarināṭṭuṭaṉ iṇintirunta pāṇṭināṭṭil, iv vaḻakkam iṉṟum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. atu kātu vaḷarttal eṉappaṭum. āṭavar tam cōṇittaṇṭi attuṇi nīṭṭāviṭiṉum, kuḻiyum kuṇṭalamum kuṇṭuk kaṭukkaṉum aṇiyumaḷavu tuḷiyi akalittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uḻavuttoḻil varavara vaḷarntu muḻunēra vuḻippiyum vēṇṭiyamiyālum, ataṟkup pala putuk karuvikaḷ vēṇṭi yiruntamiyālum, vāḻkkiyilum nākarikam vaḷaravaḷarap putitu putitākap pala tēvikaḷ tōṉṟiyamiyālum, ellārkkum ellā vēliyuñ ceyya nēramilli yeṉṟum; nēramiruppiṉum, viruppaṅkaḷum tiṟamikaḷum iyaṟkiyākavē makkaṭku vēṟu paṭṭiruppatāl, ellārum ellā vēliyuñ ceyya viyalā teṉṟum kaṇṭapōtu, iyaṟkiyākavē paṟpala toḻiṟ pātīṭukaḷ vevvēṟu kālattil ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
marutanila vāḻkkiyil, aḷavāṉa ūrtoṟum, mutaṉmutaṟ kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya oru pūcakaṉum, kāval ceytu vaḻakkut tīrkka oru talivaṉum, paṇṭamāṟṟa oru kaṭikkāraṉun tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, uḻavarkku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷic ceytutavap pakkat tuṇiyākap pala toḻilāḷar paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. avarip patiṉeṇkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatu marapu.  ak kuṭikaḷi viṉcilō (Winslow) akaramutali piṉvarumāṟu kūṟum:

kaṉṉāṉ cempuk kālattilum, iruppuk kollaṉ irumpuk kālattilumē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
		tuṭiyaṉ pāṇaṉ paṟiyaṉ kaṭampaṉeṉ
		ṟinnāṉ kallatu kuṭiyu milli	(puṟam.335)
eṉṉum  puṟanāṉūṟṟuk  kūṟṟu,  oru  kuṟippiṭṭa  iṭampaṟṟiyatē yaṉṟip potuppaṭak kūṟiyataṉṟu.
kuṭimakkaḷ eṉṉuñ col, niliyāṉa kuṭikaḷākiya uḻavark kup pala toḻiluñ ceytu makkaḷpōl utavupavar eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatu. kuṭimakkaḷ ellāruḷḷum vitappākak kuṭimakaṉ eṉṟu iṉṟuñ collappaṭupavaṉ muṭitiruttāḷaṉē.
kuṭimakkaḷ palarum tam toḻiṟku allatu toṇṭiṟku uriya kūliyik kaḷattilum uḻavarmaṉiyilum vāṅki vantaṉar.
vayalilum vāykkālilum mīṉ piṭikkappaṭumēṉum, kaṭuṅ kōṭik kālattil āṟuṅ kuḷamum vaṟṟiviṭumātalālum, kaḷar nilattu uppiṉum kaṭaluppē ciṟantatākiyālum, cempaṭavarum umaṇarum marutatti yaṭutta neytal nilattil taṅki, muṟiyē 
mīṉ piṭittum uppu viḷittum varuvārāyiṉar. avar kuṭiyirup piṟkuk kuppam eṉṟum tuṟi yeṉṟum peyar.
paḻaṅ kaṟkālattiṟ paṭaveḻuttum (Pictograph), putuk kaṟ kālattiṟ karutteḻuttum (Ideograph) tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatu  muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. paṭaveḻuttāvatu, oru ceyki yip paṭamākavē varintu kāṭṭuvatu. karutteḻuttāvatu, ovvoru  karuttiyum paṭaveḻut taṭippaṭiyil oru kuṟiyāṟ kuṟippatu.
poṟkālattil aciyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. aciyeḻuttāvatu (Syllabary), uyirmeyyi uyirum meyyumākap pirikkāmal taṉiyeḻuttāka eḻutuvatu.
paḻaṅ kaṟkālattil pattuvariyum, putuk kaṟkālattil nūṟu variyum, poṟkālattil āyiramvariyum, kumari māntar eṇṇat terintirukkalām.
kūṭṭūrāṭci niliyil, marutanilat talivaṉ ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ (makiṇaṉ) eṉṟum, mullinilat talivaṉ aṇṇal, tōṉṟal, kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ eṉṟum, kuṟiñcinilat talivaṉ veṟpaṉ, cilampaṉ, poruppaṉ eṉṟum, neytalnilat talivaṉ tuṟivaṉ, cērppaṉ, koṇkaṉ, mellam pulampaṉ eṉṟum patavippeyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. makiḻnaṉ eṉpatu, marutanilattu āṭalpāṭalik kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum piṟa iṉpaṅkaḷi nukarntum makiḻvuṟṟavaṉ eṉpati yuṇarttum. kuṟunila vāṭciniliyil, aracar aṉivarum maṉṉar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
3. cempukkālam (Copper Age)
(tōrā. ki. mu. 30,000-15,000)
poṉkiṭippu varavarak kuṉṟiyatālum, aṇikalaṉkaṭkum uṇkalaṅkaṭkum kuṭikalaṅkaṭkumē poṉṉip peritum payaṉ paṭuttiyatālum, nāḷaṭiviṟ pala karuvikaḷiyuṅ kalaṅkaḷiyum ceytaṟkēṟṟa cempik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar.
		kuykkoḷ koḻuntuvi neyyuṭi yaṭicil
		maticēr nāṇmīṉ pōla naviṉṟa
		ciṟupoṉ naṉkalañ cuṟṟa irīik
		kēṭiṉ ṟāka pāṭunar kaṭumpeṉa
		aritupeṟu polaṅkalam eḷitiṉiṉ vīci
		naṭṭōr naṭṭa nallicik kumaṇaṉ	(puṟam.140)
eṉṉum  piṟkālac ceyyuḷ muṟkāla  nilimiyiyum uṇarttum.
poruni yeṉṉum nelli māvaṭṭa āṟṟaṅkariyiṟ cempu kiṭittatāl, av vāṟu tāmparac ceppu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tāmiraparaṇi eṉṉum irupiṟappic collāl (hybrid) piṟkālattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. nelli yarukiluḷḷa oru nakar ceppaṟi (cempu + aṟi) eṉap peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. civaperu māṉiṉ aivaki ampalaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa nelli yampalam, ceppōṭu vēyap peṟṟatāl ceppampalam (tāmiracapi) eṉappaṭṭatu. potiyamali cempiṟ poruppu eṉappaṭṭatu.
		teṉkāl viṭukkuñ   cempiṟ poruppu   	(kallā.51:11)
cempiṟ poruppu = cemputtātu uḷḷa mali. itic ceppuvari yeṉṟu kuṟṟālat talapurāṇaṅ kūṟum.
cenniṟamāyiruntataṉāl, cempu eṉṟum cempoṉ eṉṟum tāmparam eṉṟum, putitāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa māḻi allatu poṉṉam peyar peṟṟatu.

paṭṭun tukilu muṭuttu 			(nālaṭi.264)

tumparam - tamparam - tāmparam = cempu (patārtta.1170)
tāmparam -t. tāmra.
tamparam - tampar = veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil 					(māṟaṉalaṅ.470, utā.)
tamparam=tampalam=1. veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil. tillinal lārpotut tampalaṅ koṇarntō (tirukkō.396) bj.j«k. 2.veṟṟili pākku. tiyāl tampalan tiṉṟiyō (kalit.65) .3. tampalap pūcci.
tampalam - va. tāmpūla.
tampalap pūcci = tampalampōṟ civanta mūtāy.
tampalam - tampal = veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil.
veḷḷilit tampal 		    (kamparā. varikkā.49)
tampalam - tampali = civanta ilantip paḻam, atu paḻukkum 
muṭceṭi.
tumar - tamar - tamari = tāmari = cemmuḷari.
tāmari eṉṉuñ col iṉṟu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiḻantu, potupporuḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, cemmuḷariyik kuṟikkac centāmari eṉṟu mikipaṭak kūṟalākac colla vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. itu, ariñāṇ koṭi, kuḷirnta taṇṇīr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻu vaḻakkē.
tāmari - va. tāmarasa.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, vaṭacollākak karutappaṭum tāmparam, tāmari eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum teṉcollē yeṉṟum, iṅṅaṉamē nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil iṉammaṟintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, tamiḻ uṇmiyil tiraviṭat tāyum āriya mūlamum ākumeṉṟum  aṟintukoḷka.
poṟkālattiṟ poṉṉāl uṇkalam kuṭikalam nīrkkalam mutaliya palvakik kalaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavēṉum, avi perumakkaḷālēyē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṉ
mutalākap potumakkaḷ puḻaṅkiya māḻik kalaṅkaḷ, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaviyē. ataṉāl, cila kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭiviyuṭiya kalaṅkaḷ  evvek karuviyāṟ ceyyappaṭiṉum, iṉṟum cempu, ceppu, ceppukkuṭam eṉac cempukkālap peyarkaḷālēyē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
niliyāka vittup pōṟṟa vēṇṭiya āvaṇaṅkaḷum muṟikaḷum, eṅkum eḷitāka eṭuttuc cellakkūṭiya vakiyil, ceppēṭukaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuc ceppup paṭṭiyam eṉap
paṭṭaṉa.
cempil vārkkappaṭṭa teyvap paṭimikaḷ cempuk kuṭṭi yeṉṟum, cepput tirumēṉi yeṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
		puḷiyiṭṭa cempiyum pōṟṟu kilēṉuyar poṉṉeṉavē
			(paṭṭiṉattār,potu.61)
mutaṉmutal marattiṟ kuṭiyappaṭṭa allatu ceyyappaṭṭa marakkāl eṉṉum mukattalaḷavik karuvi, cempilum vārkkap paṭṭuc ceppukkāl eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ceppukkāl tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyāṉ eṉpatu, cōḻar kālattil vaḻaṅkiya nellaḷakkuṅ karuvi vaki.
uḷi, katti, aram, vāḷ, kaṟaṇṭi, kalappik koḻu mutaliya palavakik karuvikaḷum, poṟkālattiṟkup piṉ cempilēyē ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
valiya pakivarālum eḷitāyt takarkka muṭiyāvāṟu, cempi yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiya iñci yeṉṉuṅ kōṭṭi matilvaki, erumiyūr(micūr) nāṭṭut tuvari nakari liruntami mēṟkuṟikkappaṭṭatu. irāvaṇaṉ kōṭṭi attakiya matiluṭimiyāṟ ceppukkōṭṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
	cempiṭṭuc ceyta iñcit tirunakarc celvam tēṟi
			(kamparā.kumpa.160)
neytalnila māntar kaṭṭumaram, timil, paṭaku mutaliya kalaṅkaḷic celutti āḻkaṭalil mīṉ piṭittatoṭu, calaṅkukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu muttuk kuḷittum pavaḻat tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟu pavaḻaṅ koṇarn tum tam vāḻkkiyi vaḷampaṭuttiṉar. kiḷiñcil muttumāli kaḷic celvarum, valampuri iṭampuri muttumālikaḷi aracarum vāṅkiyaṇintaṉar. caṅku vaḷiyalum pavaḻamāliyum peṇṭirāl virumpiyaṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. cippi nīṟṟuc cuṇṇam, ciṟanta veṇ cutiyākap  payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
oruvaṉ oruttiyoṭu maṟivākak kūṭiya piṉ avaḷoṭu kūṭavilli yeṉṟu maṟutturittalum, avaḷik kiviṭutalum, oruvaṉ oruttiyoṭu valintu kūṭutalum ākiya poyyum oḻukkak kēṭum palariṭit tōṉṟiyapiṉ, peṟṟōriṉ icivu peṟṟē oru peṇṇik koḷḷumāṟum, irupālārum veḷip
paṭiyākak kaṟpuṭaṉ oḻukumāṟum, viḻāvoṭu kūṭiya karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkip periyōr ēṟpaṭutti vittaṉar.

itaṉ poruḷ:1-4.uḻuttam paruppoṭu cērttuc camitta koḻumiyāṉa kuḻinta poṅkalōṭu periya cōṟṟut tiraḷiyi uṇṭal iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻa, variciyāṉa kāl kaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaṟkīḻk koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiya maṇalip parappi, vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi, mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu,
5-10. tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkiya vaḷinta veṇṇi lāvik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiyuṭiya cakaṭam eṉṉum nāḷ aṭiya, mikunta iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟ kāliyil, uccantaliyiṟ kuṭattiyum kiyiṟ putiya akaṉṟa montiyiyum uṭiya, maṇañ ceytu vikkum āravāramuḷḷa mutiya maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum piṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum muṟippaṭi eṭutteṭuttuk koṭukka,       
11-16. makaṉip peṟṟa tēmaluḷḷa aḻakiya vayiṟṟiyum tūya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭiniṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum tavaṟātu pala naṟpēṟukaḷip peṟṟu, uṉ kaṇavaṉ virumpip pēṇum viruppattiṟ kiṭamākuka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīroṭu cērttup peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ, aṭarnta kariya kūntalil nelloṭu viḷaṅka,
17. nalla maṇaviḻā muṭinta piṉpu.

	itaṉ poruḷ:1-9. neñcē, kuṟṟamaṟap paruppuṭaṉ kalan tākkiya neymikunta veṇcōṟṟi, nīkkāta īkit taṉmiyuṭaṉ uyarnta cuṟṟattār mutaliyōri uṇpittu, puṭkuṟi iṉitākak  kūṭa, teḷḷiya oḷiyiyuṭiya aḻakiya iṭamakaṉṟa periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamaṟa viḷaṅka, tiṅkaḷic cakaṭam kūṭiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉṉāḷil, maṇamaṉiyic cuvaṭittuk kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭu, maṇamēḷattuṭaṉ periya muracam muḻaṅka, talivikku maṇanīrāṭṭiya makaḷir, tam kūriya kaṇkaḷāl imiyātu nōkki maṟiya,  
10-18. melliya pūviyuṭiya vākiyiṉ aḻakaṟṟa piṉ puṟattik koṇṭa kavartta iliyi mutiya kaṉṟu kaṟitta paḷḷattiṟ paṭarnta aṟukiṉ, iṭi muḻaṅkiya vāṉattu mutaṉ maḻikku arumpiya kaḻuviya nīlamaṇipōlum kariya itaḻiyuṭiya pāvi yotta kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṇamuḷḷa arumpuṭaṉ cērttuk kaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūlic cūṭṭi, tūya puttāṭiyāṟ poliyac ceytu, viruppattuṭaṉ kūṭi, maḻiyōci pōṉṟa maṇavōci mikunta pantalil aṇikaḷi mikutiyāy aṇin tiruntataṉāl uṇṭāṉa viyarviyi viciṟiyāl āṟṟi, avaḷ cuṟṟattār avaḷi namakkut tanta mutal nāḷ iravil.
ivi piṟkālattaṉavāyiṉum, pirāmaṇaṉum camaṟkiruta mantiramuṅ kalavāta paṇṭit tūya tamiḻat tirumaṇa viḻāviṉ iyalpit teḷivākak kāṭṭuvaṉavākum.
marutatti yaṭuttu nāṭṭiṟ kaṇittāka vuḷḷa mulli nila vāṇar, āṭumāṭerumi yeṉṉum munniriyiyum, ciṟappāka āniriyi vaḷarppati mutaṉmit toḻilākavum, vāṉāvārip payir viḷivippatit tuṇittoḻilākavuṅ koṇṭu, piṟarkkut tīṅku ceyyātum ēṟu taḻuvi maṇakkum maṟaviyali mēṟkoṇṭum vāḻntuvara; kuṟiñciyi yaṭuttuk kāṭṭuc cārpākavum nāṭṭiṟkut tolivilum uḷḷa mullinila vāṇarō, tam vāḻnilam āṇṭu toṟum vēṉilil vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu vempāliyāka māṟiyatāl, āṟalittaliyuñ cūṟikōṭaliyumē tam kulat toḻilākak koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar. koṭuvaṟaṭci koḷḷikkē tūṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻakam kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉa aintiṇip pākupāṭuṟṟatu. tiṇi aintāyiṉum, niliyāṉa nilam nāṉkē yātalāl, ñālam nāṉilam eṉṉappaṭṭatu. pālinilat talivar viṭali, kō, vēḷ, mīḷi, kāḷi eṉap palpeyar peṟṟaṉar.

eṉpatāl  aṟiyalām.
poṟkālattiṟ kuṟunila maṉṉavarā yiruntavaruṭ cilar cempuk kālattiṟ perunila maṉṉarāyiṉar. muṉṉavar maṉṉar eṉṟum, piṉṉavar kōkkaḷ eṉṟuñ collappaṭṭaṉar.
		kōkkaṇṭu maṉṉar kuṭikaṭal pukkilar kōkaṉakap 
		pūkkaṇṭu koṭṭiyum pūvā toḻintila   
eṉṉum taṉippāṭalāl, maṉṉaṉiṉum periyavaṉ kō eṉpati aṟika. kō eṉpatu kōvaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu. kōkkaḷi (ākkaḷi)k kākkum āyaṉ pōla makkaḷik kāppavaṉ aracaṉ eṉpatu karuttu. iv  voppumi paṟṟiyē, āyaṉ kōlpōl aracaṉum oru  kōlik kiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ. āṭci nērmiyā yirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatik kāṭṭavē, nērāṉa kōl koḷḷap paṭṭatu. kōl āṭciyik kuṟikkuñ ciṉṉamātalāl, muṟiyāṉa āṭci ceṅkōl eṉṟum, muṟitavaṟiya āṭci koṭuṅkōl eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa. koṭumi = vaḷivu. cemmi = nērmi. kōṉ eṉṉum teṉ collē turukkiyiṟ kāṉ eṉṟu tirintatākak kālṭuvelār kūṟuvar.
kōvaṉ-kōṉ-kō = aracaṉ.
	kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ iṉṟum āyaṉiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. kōṉ eṉṉuñ col ār eṉṉum uyarvup paṉmi yīṟu peṟṟuk kōṉār eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
pālinilan tavira maṟṟa nāṉku nilaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavuṅ kikkoṇṭavar kōkkaḷāvar. amiccar, paṭittalivar, pūcakar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉṉum aimperuṅ kuḻuvār avarukku āṭcit tuṇiyāyiruntaṉar.
aintiṇikaḷun tōṉṟiyapiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ kuṟiñciyil vēṭṭiyāṭuṅ kuṟavarum, mulliyil munniri vaḷarkkum iṭiyarum,marutattil uḻutoḻilic ciṟappākac ceyyum uḻavarum, pāliyil vaḻippaṟittuk koḷḷiyaṭikkum maṟavarum, neytalil mīṉpiṭikkum paṭavarum, ākap perumpālum ovvoru vakuppārē vāḻntiruppar.
ovvoru tiṇinilattilum uḷḷa ovvoru makkaṭ kuṭiyiruppilum, aṭakkiyāḷavum vaḻakkut tīrkkavum oru talivaṉ tōṉṟiyiruppaṉ. avaṉ  iṟṟi nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉpōl piṟar ceyyun toḻiliyē ceytiruppaṉ. teyva vaṇakkam allatu accam iyaṟkip paṇpātalāl, talivaṉukkup piṉ oru pūcakaṉ allatu tēvarāḷaṉun tōṉṟiyiruppaṉ. avarkkumuṉ, marutanilap pērūrkaḷil paṇṭamāṟṟiyar allatu vaṇikar tōṉṟiyiruppar. āṇṭutōṟum pala kūlaṅkaḷum putitākavum ērāḷamākavum viḷinta tāl, muntiṉa āṇṭil mīntupōṉa kūlaṅkaḷi vāṅkavum, avaṟṟiṟ kīṭāka uḻavarkku vēṇṭiya piṟanilattup poruḷkaḷik koṇṭuvantu taravum, uḻavariṉiṉṟē orucilar vaṇikarākap pirintiruttal vēṇṭum. yāṇṭukaḻi veṇṇel eṉṟu aiṅkuṟunūṟṟup pāṭal kūṟuvati nōkkuka. oṉṟiraṇṭām vāṇikam eṉpatum vaṇika muyaṟciyi ūkki yiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṇikarukkuppiṉ palvēṟu kittoḻilāḷar paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟalāyiṉar. nākarikamum araciyalum vaṇikamum  vaḷara vaḷara, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa paṇikaḷum aluval kaḷum putituputitāka ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aṟivu vaḷarcci yaṭintataṉāl, pūcakar kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyat toḻilum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. uḻavu, vaṇikam, kāval, kalvi eṉṉum nāṟperun toḻilpaṟṟi, marutanila makkaḷ uḻavar, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉa nālvakuppārāy amintaṉar. patiṉeṇ kittoḻi lāḷarum uḻavarkkup pakkat tuṇivarākak karutappaṭṭataṉāl, avaruḷ aṭakkappaṭṭaṉar.
paṇṇiyam = paṇṇappaṭṭa poruḷ, viṟpaṉip paṇṭam. 
(maturik.405)
  paṇṇiyam - paṇṇiyaṉ - paṇṇikaṉ - paṇikaṉ - vaṇikaṉ = pala paṇṭaṅkaḷi viṟpavaṉ. vaṇikaṉ - vāṇikaṉ.
   pārppāṉ = nūlkaḷip pārppavaṉ. itu aṉ cāriyi allatu  aṉaṉ īṟu peṟṟup pārppaṉaṉ eṉṟum niṟkum. mukkāla viṉimuṟṟum iṅṅaṉam īṟu koḷḷum.
e-ṭu. 	pārttaṉaṉ	     	- 	iṟanta kālam
       		pārkkiṉṟaṉaṉ 		- 	nikaḻkālam
       		pārppaṉaṉ 	    	- 	etirkālam
paṭippaṉar (cilap.10:134), virivaṉaṉ (puṟam.150) eṉṉum etirkāla viṉimuṟṟukaḷi nōkkuka. pārppaṉaṉ eṉpatu pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ tiripaṉṟu. kalvittoḻil ceyyum āciriyarum camaya kuravarum pōṉṟa tamiḻariyellām pārppāreṉṟē colla vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇari ic collāṟ kuṟikka vēṇṭiṉ, āriyap pārppār (allatu pārppaṉar) eṉṟu aṭikoṭuttē kūṟavēṇṭum.
aracar eṉṉuñ col piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
moḻivaḷarcciyil, eṇvēṟṟumikaḷum mukkālamum palvaki ecca muṟṟukaḷum, muḻuniṟivāṉa coṟṟoṭa ramippum, cempuk kālattil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. eḻuttu vakiyil oliyeḻuttut (Phonetic characters) tōṉṟiyirukkum. āyiṉ, ekara vokarak kuṟiluyi rolikaḷum ḷakara ḻakara ṟakara ṉakara mey yolikaḷum perumpālun tōṉṟiyirā. ikkālattiltāṉ, pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭa moḻiyāḷariṉ muṉṉōr vaṭa nāvalam (intiyā) ceṉṟiruppar. pāvakiyil āciriyamum kaliyum tōṉṟiyirukkum. makkaḷiṉ eṇṇuntiṟaṉ patiṉāyiramvari ceṉṟirukkum.
matattuṟiyil, maṟumiyuṇarvum viṇṇulakak koḷkiyum tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
4. uṟikkālam (Bronze Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 15,000 - 10,000)
uṟi yeṉpatu veṇkalam. itu muṟiyeṉavum paṭum.
		kañcam uṟiveṇ kalamā kummē.	(piṅ.6:140)

	veṇkalap peyarum viḻumamum perumiyum
		..........................................
		ōriṭic collum vāḻnāḷum uṟiyeṉal.	(piṅ.10:184)
eṭṭup paṅku cempum, oru paṅku takaramuṅ kalanta kalappu māḻiyē uṟi. itu cempupōlat taṉimāḻi yaṉmiyālum, veṇṇiṟak kalavaṭivilēyē makkaḷ itik kaṇṭamiyālum, karumiya (kāriya) vākupeyarāka veṇkalam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu.
cempu, kumpā, kiṇṇam, kuṭam, vaṭṭil mutaliya kalaṅkaḷum; māṭaviḷakku, kuttuviḷakku, pāviviḷakku mutaliya tiriviḷakku vakikaḷum; vāḷ, kaṟaṇṭi mutaliya karuvikaḷum uṟiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇkala ēṉattil vārtta allatu vitta nīruṅ kaṭṭiyu māṉa uṇavupporuḷkaḷ, ciṟappākap puḷippup paṇṭaṅkaḷ, kittuṅ keṭṭum pōkāvātalāl, poṉṉum veḷḷiyum »ṭiyātaVḻikntarkkumïṭittiṟatkuppārkkumbvaṇkalaVṉamēáṟantakḻikkalamākaïruntuvantatu.br¥ò¡Fl« eṉṉum nīrkkalam cempiṉāṟ peyar peṟiṉum, veṇkalatti ṉālēyē iṉṟuñ ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
vaṇkala vōci muḻaṅkuvatālum mika nīṇṭu niṟpatālum, kittāḷam, vaṇṭittāḷam, kimmaṇi, nāḻimaṇi, āṉmaṇi, kutirimaṇi, yāṉimaṇi, tērmaṇi, kōpuramaṇi, cēmakkalam, palakimaṇi mutaliya icikkaruvikaṭku uṟiyiyē mutaṟ karuvi
yākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iṭikkural muracam iḻumeṉ pāṇṭil (cilap.26:194). itiṟ pāṇṭil eṉṟatu vaṇṭit tāḷatti.
uvaccarum piṟa pūcakarum pūciyiṟ payaṉpaṭuttuvatu kimmaṇi.
		kimmaṇic cīraṉṟic cīraṟi yākkampa nāṭaṉ coṉṉa
		mummaṇik kōvi mutaṟcīr piḻi...........
eṉṟu vāṇiyaṉ tātaṉ kamparmītu aṅkatam pāṭiṉāṉ.
		tīṅkaṉi yiravamoṭu vēmpumaṉic cerīi
		vāṅkumarup piyāḻoṭu palliyaṅ kaṟaṅkak
		kipayap peyarttu miyiḻu tiḻuki
		aiyavi citaṟi āmpa lūti
		icimaṇi yeṟintu kāñci pāṭi
		neṭunakar varippiṟ kaṭinaṟi pukiik
		kākkam vammō kātalan tōḻi
		vēntuṟu viḻuman tāṅkiya
		pūmpoṟik kaḻaṟkāl neṭuntaki puṇṇē	(puṟam.281) 
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭil icimaṇiyeṉṟatu kimmaṇiyiyē.
		kaṟaṅkumaṇi tuvikkum ēṟuṭip peruniri	(malipaṭu.573)
eṉpatil kuṟittatu āṉmaṇi.
		kaṟaṅkumaṇi vāluḷip puraviyoṭu	(ciṟupāṇ.91-2)
eṉpatil  kuṟittatu kutirimaṇi.
		kayiṟupiṇik koṇṭa kaviḻmaṇi maruṅkiṉ
		peruṅki yāṉiyirum piṭarttali yiruntu      	(puṟam.3:10-11)
eṉpatil kuṟittatu yāṉimaṇi.
		pūtta poṅkart tuṇiyoṭu vatinta
		tātuṇ paṟavi pētuṟa lañci
		maṇinā ārtta māṇviṉit tēraṉ	(akam4:10-12)
eṉpatil  kuṟittatu tērmaṇi.
yāṉiyi maṇi kaṭṭātu teruvaḻic cella viṭuvatilli. ataṉāl, yāṉivarum piṉṉē, maṇiyōci varum muṉṉē eṉṟa paḻamoḻi yeḻuntatu.
		maṇiyilāk kuñcaram vēntūrta liṉṉā	(iṉṉā.14) 
eṉṟār kapilar.
vaḻakkiḻantō peruntīṅku ceyyappaṭṭō aracaṉiṭattil muṟiyiṭuvōr acikkumāṟu, araṇmaṉi vāyiliṟ kaṭṭap paṭṭa ārāycci maṇi veṇkala maṇiyē.
		vāyiṟ kaṭimaṇi naṭunā naṭuṅka
		āviṉ kaṭimaṇi yukunīr neñcucuṭat tāṉṟaṉ
		arumpeṟaṟ putalvaṉi yāḻiyiṉ maṭittōṉ	(cilap.20:53-5)
		āṭuṅ kaṭimaṇi nāvaci yāmal akilameṅkum
		nīṭuṅ kuṭiyit tarittapi rāṉinta nīṇilattil
		pāṭum pulavar pukaḻoṭṭak kūttaṉ patāmpuyattic
	cūṭuṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉeṉ ṟēyeṉic collu varē eṉpatu, oṭṭakkūttarum iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉum muṉṉīraṭiyum piṉṉīraṭiyumākap pāṭiya taṉippāṭal.
potumakkaḷ nēramaṟiyumāṟu,  nāḻikitōṟum aṭikkak kōpurattiṟ kaṭṭiya perumaṇi kōpura maṇiyām.
uṟutiyum pārviyuḷḷatum, pōtiya aḷavu kiṭikkak kūṭiyatum, perumpāluṅ kaḷavuṅ koḷḷiyum pōkātatum āṉa māḻi uṟiyē yātalāl, atilēyē ciṟiyavum periyavumāṉa teyvap paṭimikaḷum makkaṭpaṭimikaḷum vārkkappaṭṭaṉa.
uṟikkālattil neytalnila makkaḷ kappal, nāvāy mutaliya peruṅkalaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, akkari nāṭukaḷilum avaṟṟiyaṭutta tīvukaḷilumuḷḷa palvaki arumpaṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuvantu nīrvāṇikattip perukkiṉar.
āṭcittuṟiyil kōviṉum periya vēntaṉ tōṉṟi, aintiṇi yaracariyum aṭakki yāṇṭāṉ. taṉakkoru taṉic ciṟappu vēṇṭi, muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyit taṉakkē koṇṭāṉ. itaṉālēyē, avaṉ vēntaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
mē = mēl. mē - mēy. mēytal = vilaṅku pulliṉ mēṟ pakutiyit tiṉṉutal, kūriyiṉmēl vikkōliṭutal.
mēy-vēy. vēytal=kūriyiṉmēl vikkōliṭutal, 	oṟṟaṉ oru kōlatti mēṟkoṇṭu uḷavaṟital, talimēl 	makuṭamaṇital.
vēy - vēyntōṉ = makuṭamaṇintōṉ.
vēyntōṉ - vēyntaṉ - vēntaṉ.
koṉṟivēyntōṉ = koṉṟi māliyi allatu malarit taliyiṟ cūṭiya civaṉ.
koṉṟivēyntōṉ - koṉṟivēntaṉ = civaṉ.
vēntaṉ-vēntu. o.nō:aracaṉ - aracu.
amiccaṉ - amiccu, pāṅkaṉ - pāṅku, pārppāṉ - pārppu.
muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
vaṇpoḻil mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ	(1336)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ  kūṟuvatāl ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari ic ciṟappurimi kākkappaṭṭu vantateṉa aṟiyalām. ataṉpiṉ, kaṭik kaḻaka kālattil kuṟunila maṉṉar tali yeṭuttatāl, mūvēntarum iv vatikāratti iḻantuviṭṭaṉar.
		paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
		kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
		vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
		teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi 	(cilap.11:19-22)
eṉṟu   iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ   pāṭuvatālum,   tamiḻaṉ   piṟantakam kumari nāṭātalālum, kumarikkaṇṭat  tamiḻnila   muḻutum  paḻam pāṇṭināṭātalālum, mūvēntaruḷḷum mutalil  tōṉṟi yavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉē  eṉpatu   aṟiyappaṭum.   piṟkālattil, nāvalan tēyattiṉ kīḻppākattiyum  mēṟpākattiyum  tuṇiyaracarāka  allatu  maṇṭilat   talivarāka   āḷumāṟu    amarttappeṟṟa   pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭiyiṉar iruvarē, cēracōḻarāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭi = vaṇṭi(cakkaram, cakaṭam)
paṇṭi - pāṇṭi = 1. vaṭṭamāṉa viḷiyāṭṭuc cil.
2. atikkoṇṭu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu (vaṭṭāṭṭu)
3. māṭṭu vaṇṭi. "akavarum pāṇṭiyum" (paripā.10:16)
4. kūṭārappaṇṭi. (cilap. 14:168, arum.)
5. (uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa) erutu (paripā.20:17, kuṟippu)
		o.nō: kuṇṭu - kuṇṭi = erutu.
pāṇṭi - pāṇṭiyam = 1. erutu. ``ceñcuvaṟ pāṇṭiyam"
		(peruṅ. uñcik .38:32)
6. (erutukoṇṭu  uḻum) uḻavu. "pāṇṭiyañ ceyvāṉ                         		    poruḷiṉum" (kalit. 136)
pāṇṭi - pāṇṭil = 1. vaṭṭam. "polampacum pāṇṭiṟ kācu" (aiṅ.310). 2. vaṭṭakkaṭṭil. "pēraḷa veytiya perum
peyarp pāṇṭil" (neṭunal. 123).  3. vaṭṭattōl. "puḷḷi yiralit tōlū ṉutirttut tītukaḷin teytiya tikaḻviṭu pāṇṭil" (patiṟ. 74), 4. vaṭṭattāḷam. "iṭikkuraṉ muraca miḻumeṉ pāṇṭil" (cilap.26:194), 5. vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi. "oḷirum..... pāṇṭil" (pu.ve.6:12)  6. vaṭṭakkiṇṇi. " pāṇṭi leṭutta paḵṟāmari kīḻum paḻaṉaṅkaḷē" (tirukkō.249). 7. viḷakkut takaḻi (piṅ.). 8. tērvaṭṭi  (cilap.12:168, uri), 9. irucakkara vaṇṭi. "viyamum pāṇṭilum"(cilap.14:168), 10. kutiri pūṭṭiya tēr (tivā.). 11. nāṭu. o.nō: maṇṭalam, vaṭṭāram. 12. erutu, kāḷi.
kāḷi maṟam viñciyatātaliṉ, pōrmaṟavaṉ kāḷi yeṉap paṭṭāṉ.
kāḷi =1. iḷaverutu. 2. kaṭṭiḷam paruvattiṉaṉ (tivā.). 3. pālinilat talivaṉ(tivā.). 4. pōr maṟavaṉ. "uravuvēṟ 	kāḷiyum"(puṟam.334).
kāḷi maṟam viñciyatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, kaṟpāṟiyilum āṟṟu maṇalilum cēṟṟu nilattilum mēṭṭilum paḷḷattilum poṟivaṇṭiyi "maruṅkoṟṟi mūkkūṉṟit tāḷ tavaḻntu" iḻuttuc celluṅ kaṭippiṭiyumuḷḷatu. ataṉāl,
		maṭuttavā yellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa 
		iṭukkaṇ iṭarppā ṭuṭittu	(kuṟaḷ.624)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. 
		accoṭu tākkip pāruṟ ṟiyaṅkiya 
		paṇṭac cākāṭ ṭāḻcci colliya
		varimaṇaṉ ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum
		perumitap pakaṭṭukkut tuṟiyu muṇṭō	(puṟam.90)
eṉṟār auviyār.
aracaṉ pōrmaṟamum āṭcittiṟaṉum oruṅkē yuṭiyavaṉ eṉpati yuṇarttaṟku, kumarinila mutal tamiḻa vēntaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉak kuṭippeyar peṟṟāṉ. ceḻiyaṉ, vaḻuti, māṟaṉ mutaliya kuṭippaṭṭaṅkaḷum piṉṉart tōṉṟiṉa. pāṇṭināṭu veppa nāṭātalāl, kuḷirveṇmati kulamutalākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu pōlum! vēppantāri yaṇintataṟkum kayaṟkoṭi koṇṭa
taṟkum ituvē karaṇiyamā yirukkalām. tārālum koṭiyālum vēmpaṉ mīṉavaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum eḻuntaṉa. kayalmīṉ yāṉimīṉum paṉimīṉum pōlap parumīṉaṉṟātaliṉ, atik koṉṟataṉāṟ piṭitta veṟṟicciṉṉamākak kayaṟkoṭiyik karuta iṭamilli. kuḷirnta pēroḷi vīcum muḻumati pōṉṟavaṉ, kuṭikaḷ pēriṉpamum aṟivum peṟa āḷum aracaṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṟ piṭikkappaṭṭa mativaṭṭakkuṭi allatu veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyum tamiḻakattiṉ, ciṟappākap pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ veppa niliyik kuṟippāka vuṇarttum.
naṇṇilakkōṭu kumarimuṉikkut teṟkiṟ patiṉ pākikkuḷ iruppatum, iṟṟit tamiḻakattilum vaṭavāṇariṉum teṉvāṇar perumpāluṅ karuttiruppatum, muḻukippōṉa teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ vemmi mikutiyi yuyttuṇara ētuvākum.
cōḻa pāṇṭiyar pāṇṭavarkkut tuṇiyākavum cēraṉ naṭuniliyākavum pāratappōriṟ kalantukoṇṭataṉālum, mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum pāṇṭava kuravarkku muṉpē varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāt toṉmutu paḻaṅkālattil tōṉṟiyamiyālum, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum collip pāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉuñ col liṉiṉṟu tirippatu, varalāṟṟaṟivum ārāyccittiṟaṉum illātavar ceyaleṉak kūṟi     viṭukka.
iṉi, paḻiyaṉ eṉṉum kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻntu, vēmpik kāvalmaramāka vaḷarttatiyum, pāṇṭiya vēntariṉ paḻimiyiyum nōkki, paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉuñ collit tirippar cilar. pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ iṉṟutāṉ namakkup paḻimiyāṉavaṉē yaṉṟi, avaṉ mutaṉmutalākak kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiyapōtu oruvarukkum paḻimiyāṉavaṉ allaṉ eṉṟum, paḻiyaṉ eṉṉuñ col oruvaṉatu iyaṟpeyarukkē yaṉṟi, ōr āḷkuṭip peyarukku ēṟkā teṉṟum, avar aṟital vēṇṭum.
pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ aintiṇi nilaṅkaḷiyuṅ kippaṟṟi āṇṭataṉāl, karuṅkaṭal mutal karumalivari nāṭu muḻutuṅ kāvaṟkuṭpaṭṭatu. pālinilavāṇar vēntaṉiṉ paṭi maṟavarāyiṉar. annilat talivaṉ kāṭṭuppaṭit talivaṉāṉāṉ. ēṟkeṉavē yirunta marutanilap paṭi nāṭṭuppaṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu. pōrk kālattilum amitik kālattilum niliyāka virunta paṭi nilippaṭi yeṉṟum, avvappōtu pōriṉ tēvikkut takkavāṟu kūlik kamarttappaṭṭa paṭi kūlippaṭi yeṉṟum, peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
kāval mikuntu kaḷavuṅ koḷḷiyum pōrum nīṅkavē, nāṭṭil amitiyum iṉpamum nilaviṉa. toḻilkaḷ perukiṉa. vaḻippōkkum iṭampeyarvum accam nīṅkiṉa. vaṇikam vaḷarntatu. makkaḷ perukap peruka mella mella vaṭakkē ceṉṟu paraviṉar. nāṭṭuc celvatti mikukkavum, nākarika vāḻkkikkēṟṟa poruḷkaḷit tokukkavum, kāṭṭuvaḻikaḷi lellām kāvaṟ paṭiyi niṟuvi nila vāṇikattiyum, kaṭaṟkari vaḷintuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilellām tuṟinakarkaḷi yamittu nīrvāṇikattiyum, vēntaṉ ūkkiṉāṉ.
ēṟkeṉavē, iyaṟki moḻik kālattiṟ pirintu ceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ āppirikka āttirēliya nilappakutikaḷilum, iḻittal moḻiyiṉ acinilik kālattiṟ pirintu ceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ cīṉa maṅkōliya nilappakutikaḷilum, koḷuvu nilit toṭakkak kālattiṟ pirintu ceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ mēliyāciya nilappakutikaḷilum, cempukkālattil ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ kuṟuku muṉṉum ḷa ḻa ṟa ṉa tōṉṟumuṉṉum pirintu pōṉa māntariṉaṅkaḷ vaṭintiyāvilum, perukip paravi vantaṉa.  perunilaṅkaḷil vāḻntavaruṭ cilar avaṟṟiyaṭutta ciṟunilaṅ kaḷāṉa tīvukaḷiluṅ kuṭiyēṟiṉar.

cāttar tam vaṇikac carakkukaḷi ēṟṟic cella mutaliṟ potiyerutukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. piṉṉar mēliyāciyāvi ṉiṉṟu kaḻuti, kutiri, oṭṭakam, kōvēṟu kaḻuti ākiya vaṟṟip paṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuvantu paḻakkiṉar.
kaḻutiyiṉ piṟappiṭam, vaṭakiḻakku āppirikkāvil etti yōppiyā cōmāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷum, mēliyāciyāvil ciriyā pāracīkam peluccittāṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷum, naṭu āciyāvil tipēt tum maṅkōliyāvum ākum.
kutiriyiṉ piṟappiṭam āciyāviṉ vaṭanaṭuppāka meṉṟum, aṅkiruntu atu kiḻakkē cīṉa maṅkōliya nāṭukaṭkum, mēṟkē airōppāviṟkum, teṉmēṟkē pāracīkam arapiyā mutaliya nāṭu kaṭkum, ceṉṟatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.

arapik kutirikaḷ periyaṉavum pēṇutaṟ kariyaṉavumātalāl, perumpālum paṭikaṭkum aracar ūrtaṟkumē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭ ṭirukkum. nāṭṭuttaṭṭu eṉṟum accimaṭṭam eṉṟum collap paṭum ciṟutarak kutirikaḷiyē cāttukaḷ payaṉpaṭutti yirukkum. nāṭṭuttaṭṭu intiyāvilēyē vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu. accimaṭṭam cumaturāt (Sumatra) tīviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyākiya acciyiliruntu (Achin) vantatu.
cāttukaḷ kutirikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyati, peruñcāli vaḻikaḷil āṅkāṅkuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum cāttaṉār (aiyaṉār) kōviṟkumuṉ, cutiyālum cuṭumaṇṇālum  ceytu niṟuttap paṭṭuḷḷa kutiriyuruvaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟintukoḷḷalām.
kutiri tamiḻakattiṟku vanta piṉṉarē, kumarar kātal vāḻvil maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum maṇamuṟi viṉi tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ōr iḷiñaṉ tāṉ kātalitta peṇṇi avaḷ peṟṟōr tara iciyāviṭiṉ, nīrccīli maṭṭum aṇintu uṭampu muḻutum cāmpaṟpūci erukkumāli yaṇintu, ūr naṭuccantiyiṟ paṉaṅkarukku  maṭṭiyāṟ ceyta kutirimē lamarntu, tāṉ kātalitta peṇṇiṉ uruvappaṭatti valakkiyilēnti, ati uṟṟu nōkkiyavaṇṇamā yiruppāṉ. ūrpperumakkaḷ ataṉip pārttavuṭaṉēyē, avaṉ ellā vakiyilum peṇṇiṟkut takutiyuḷḷava ṉeṉṟum avaṉiluñ ciṟantavaṉ illiyeṉṟuṅ kāṇiṉ, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōriṭam pēcit tirumaṇattiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceytirukkavuṅ kūṭum. atu attuṇi eḷitāka muṭivatā yillāviṭiṉ, avar avaṉi nōkki, nī āyvu tarukiṉṟiyā? eṉa viṉavuvar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, avaṉi am maṭaṟ kutirimītu amarntirunta vāṟē perunteru vaḻiyāka ūric cuṟṟi yiḻuppar. paṉaṅkarukkāṟ kāyam paṭuntoṟuṅ kātal maṟam (vīriyam) kiḷariṉ, avaṉ kaṭip piṭiyiyum avaṉ eṇṇam niṟi vēṟāviṭiṉ iṟantupaṭum nilimiyiyum uṉṉi, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōri vaṟpuṟutti icivittu maṇatti muṭittuvippar. itu ikkālattu uṇṇā nōṉpum pāṭukiṭappum (cattiyāk
kirakamum) pōṉṟatu. paḻiya vuriyiluḷḷa vīriyam eṉṉum coṟku vintu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷuvatu poruntātu.
peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr ūrip pakittukkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. ataṉāl, maṭalēṟṟattāṟ kātalaṉ karutiyatu kikūṭum vāyppuṇṭu. āyiṉ, atē camiyattil, kātalaṉ taṉmāṉattit tuṟakkavum nōvit tāṅkavum kāyam mikiṉ uyiri iḻakkavum aṇiya māyiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṉ arumi nōkkiyē ellārum iti mēṟkoḷvatilli. cilar veṟṟaraṭṭāka viḷampic collaḷavilēyē niṉṟuviṭuvar.
im maṭalēṟṟa vaḻakku nākarikam nirampāta paḻaṅkālattiṟ kuriyatātalāl, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē aṟṟup pōyiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, iṉṟum oruvar kōvi pāṭiṉ, maṭalūrtalum oru tuṟiyāka amital vēṇṭumeṉpatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākum. ātalāl, ayalnāṭṭār akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ kaḷip paṭittavuṭaṉ paṇṭit tamiḻ nākarikattippaṟṟit tavaṟāṉa eṇṇaṅ koḷḷāvāṟu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vaḻippaṭṭa taṉit tamiḻāciriyariṭamē tamiḻ payilumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. kōṭaṉ mārum viyāpurikaḷum tamiḻip paḻippatiluṅ keṭuppatilum āriyariṉum viñciyavarāvar.

tē-tī=1. neruppu. vaḷittaliiya tīyum (puṟam.2).  2.viḷakku. tītturīi yaṟṟu, (kuṟaḷ. 929). 3.vayiṟṟuttī, 	kaṭumpaci. vayiṟṟuttīt taṇiya (puṟam. 74). 4.ciṉattī, ciṉam. 	maṉṉartī yīṇṭutaṅ kiḷiyōṭu merittiṭum (cīvaka.250).  	 5. tīyiṉ taṉmi, tīmi.tīppāla tāṉpiṟarkaṭ ceyyaṟka(kuṟaḷ. 206). 6. nañcu. vēkaven tīnākam (maṇimē.20:98).

tēy - tēyvu. tēvu = ka. teyvam (piṅ.) narakarit tēvucey vāṉum (tēvā. 699:2). 2. teyvat taṉmi.
tēvu - tēvaṉ = kaṭavuḷ. oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum (tiruman.104). tēvaṉ - va. tēva.
tēyvu - teyvu - teyvam = 1. vaṇaṅkappaṭum poruḷ. teyva muṇāvē (tol.poruḷ.18). 2. teyvat taṉmi. 	3. teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷatu. 4. kaṭavuḷ. 5. kaṭavuḷ ēṟpāṭu, 	Cḻ.bjŒt« - va. tiva.
tēy eṉṉum mūlattiluḷḷa yakaramey, teyvam eṉṉuñ 	collilu miruttal kāṇka.
kuṟiñci nilattār, teyvam tīvaṭiviṉatu' allatu tīyottatu eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉarēṉum, teyvam allatu tēvaṉ eṉpatu potuppeyarākiviṭṭataṉāl, tīyippōṟ civantavaṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttil tam tēvaṉic cēyōṉ eṉṟaṉar. avaṉ tammippōl maṟavaṉāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karuti, maṟattiṟkuc ciṟanta iḷamip peyarāl avaṉi murukaṉ eṉṟaṉar. muruku iḷami. aḻaku eṉpatu ataṉ vaḻipporuḷ. tam paṭikkalamākiya vēli yēntiyum, kuṟiñcikkuriya kaṭappa māliyi yaṇintum iruppatākak karuti, vēlaṉ eṉṟum kaṭampaṉ  eṉṟum irupeyar cērttaṉar. mēleḻuntu viṇṇulakañ cella vutavumeṉṟu karuti, kuṟiñcik
kuriya aḻakiya perumpaṟaviyākiya mayili murukaṉukku ūrtiyākak koṇṭaṉar. avaṉ piṟa peyarkaḷum iyalkaḷum ceyalkaḷum, eṉ tamiḻar matam eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkappeṟum.
mullinilattār, tam kaṉṟukālikaṭkup pulvaḷaravum vāṉāvārik kolliyiṟ payirkaḷ viḷiyavum, iṉṟiyamiyāta maḻi peyyum karumukili allatu atu tiraḷum nīlavāṉit tam tēvaṉ vaṭivākak koṇṭu, avaṉi māyōṉ eṉṟum kariyavaṉ eṉṟum māl eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar.
marutanilattār, viṇṇulakak koḷki koṇṭataṉāl, immiyiṟ ciṟanta nalviṉi ceyyum potumakkaḷ tēvarākavum vēntaṉ tēvarkōṉākavum, maṟumiyil viṇṇulakattiṟ cērvar eṉṉum nampikkiyiṉāl, oruṅkē viṇṇulaka vēntaṉākavum maḻit teyvamākavuṅ koṇṭa tam tēvaṉi, vēntaṉ eṉṟē viḷampiṉar.
neytal nilattār, kaṭalpaṭu celvattiṟkum nīrvāṇikat tiṟkum kaṭaliyē nampiyiruntataṉāl, kaṭal talivaṉē tam tēvaṉ eṉak koṇṭu, avaṉi vāraṇaṉ eṉṟaṉar. vāraṇam = kaṭal. vāraṇaṉ kaṭal talivaṉ. vārtal = vaḷital. vaḷ-var-vār-vāraṇam. kaṭal nilattic cūḻntiruppatāl vāraṇam eṉap
paṭṭatu. vaḷikaṭal valiyiṟ cūḻntu" (cīvaka.1115). kēṭakamum caṅkum vāraṇam eṉappaṭuvatum vaḷintiruttal paṟṟiyē. thuz‹-t.tUz.fl‰nfhthuz‹ (upatē.urut. 230).
pālinilattār aṭikkaṭi āṟalittum cūṟiyāṭiyum pōriṭṭum makkaḷik koṉṟu piṇamākkiyataṉāl, ap piṇaṅ kaḷit tiṉpatākak karutappaṭṭa kūḷikaḷiṉ (pēykaḷiṉ) taliviyākiya kāḷiyi, tamakkup  pōril veṟṟi tarupavaḷākak karuti, avaḷi avar teyvamākak koṇṭu kāviṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭa ṉar. pēyniṟam karuppeṉpatu paṟṟi, avaḷ kāḷi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḷam-kāḷi=karuppi. avaḷi māyōḷ eṉpatum an niṟampaṟṟiyē. māmi = karumi. fhË-t.fhä. karuppi eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
kāḷi vaṇakkam aṭippaṭiyiṟ pēy vaṇakkamāyiṉum, atu tāy vaṇakkamum kaṇṇakipōlum kaṟpuṭit tēvi vaṇakkamuṅ kalantatākum. kāḷiyammi, koṟṟavi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ tāy vaṇakkak karuttik kāṭṭum. koṟṟam+avvi=koṟṟavi (veṟṟit tāy). iṉi, ammi yeṉṉum peyar aṭiyaṭukkātu taṉittum avaḷik kuṟikkum. ataṉāl, avaḷāl nērvatākak karutappaṭṭa koppuḷa nōy, ammi yeṉṟē peyar peṟṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam aintiṇi vaṇakkamum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, marutanilap pārppāruṭ ciṟanta aṟiñar cilar, piṟaviyaṟukkum vīṭupēṟṟuk karuttuṅkoṇṭu, ap pēṟṟip peṟum civaneṟi, tirumālneṟi ākiya iru mataṅkaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭaṉar. kuṟiñcinilattiliruntu āyntavar civaneṟiyiyum, mullinilattiliruntu āyntavar tirumāl neṟiyiyum kaṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyi mēṟkoṇṭavar, ulakap paṟṟi muṟṟat tuṟantu ellā vuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ pūṇṭamiyāl antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār.
		antaṇa reṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum 
		centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.	(kuṟaḷ. 30)
iṅṅaṉam, kalvit toḻilāḷar, illaṟam pēṇum pārppār eṉṟum, tuṟavaṟam pūṇum antaṇareṉṟum irupāṟpaṭṭaṉar. iṅ ṅaṉamē, aracarum kuṟunila maṉṉar perunila maṉṉar eṉṟum, vaṇikarum nilavaṇikar nīrvaṇikar eṉṟum, uḻavarum uḻutuṇ pōr (karuṅkaḷamar) uḻuvittuṇpōr (veṇkaḷamar) eṉṟum, ivviru tiṟappaṭṭaṉar. karuṅkaḷamar kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar veḷḷāḷar eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉar. ivviru cārārkkum vēḷāḷar eṉpatu potuppeyarākum. ēṉi mūvar pōlātu, putitāy vantavarkkum aṉpuṭaṉ viruntōmpi vēḷāṇmi ceyvataṉāl, uḻavar vēḷāḷar eṉac ciṟappikkap paṭṭaṉar. vēḷāṇmi piṟari virumpiyāḷun taṉmi.
		vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka vuṇṇātāṉ.	(tirikaṭu.12)
kuṟiñcinilattār, kuṟavar, kuṉṟavar, iṟavuḷar,kāṉavar vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum; mullinilattār, āṭṭiṭiyar, māṭṭi ṭiyar eṉṟum;neytalnilattār nuḷiyar, timilar, paratar (paravar) eṉṟum; pālinilattār eyiṉar, maṟavar, vēṭṭuvar, vēṭar eṉṟum; iṭampaṟṟiyum toḻilpaṟṟiyum karuvipaṟṟiyum ciṟcila vakuppārāyp pirintu pōyiṉar.
āyar, iṭiyar, kōvar, toṟuvar, potuvar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, peyarvēṟṟumiyē yaṉṟik kulavakuppik kuṟiyā.
āṉvallōr (tivā.) eṉṟum, āṉvallavar (cūṭā.) eṉṟum āyarkkup peyaruṇmiyāl, kōvalaṉ eṉpatu kō mēyppatil vallavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉcollē yaṉṟi, kōpālaṉ eṉṉum irupiṟappi vaṭacolliṉ tiripākātu.
pālinilam kuṟiñciyi yaṭuttatātaliṉ, vēṭṭuvar eṉṉum peyar koṇṭa vakuppār irunilattilum iruntaṉar. kaṭiya neṭuvēṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉuṅ koṭiyāḷi kōṭimalikkut talivaṉ. cilappatikārattiṟ pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷa vēṭṭuva vari pālivāṇarippaṟṟiyatu.
uṟikkālattu moḻit tuṟiyil, ekara okarak kuṟikaḷum ḷa,ḻa,ṟa,ṉa meykaḷun tōṉṟi neṭuṅkaṇakku nirampiyiruttal vēṇṭum. veṇpāvum vañcippāvun tōṉṟi nālvakip pāvilum ilakkiyam naṭipeṟṟirukkum. ici nāṭakak kali
kaḷum vaḷarcciyuṟṟu ilakkiya vilakkaṇam peṟṟirukkum. eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟu taḻuviya piṇṭa vilakkaṇamun  tōṉṟi yirukkum.
tamiḻar mīṇṭum vaṭakkē ceṉṟu paraviyiruppar.
	vaṭintiyā tiraviṭanāṭākavum,  naṭuvintiyā moḻipeyar tēyamākavum  māṟiyirukkum.
makkaḷ peruki āḷnilam mika virivaṭintuviṭṭataṉāl pāṇṭiyaṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṟku vaṭakkiṟ paṉimali variyuḷḷa nilappākattiṟ kīḻppātiyi oruvaṉum mēṟpātiyi oruvaṉum āḷumāṟu, iru tuṇiyariyari amarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
veḷḷi (veṇpoṉ)
cempum veḷḷīyamuṅ kalantu  uṟiyeṉṉuṅ kalappup poṉṉam (metal)  amittataṟku muṉṉō piṉṉō veḷḷi allatu veṇpoṉ eṉṉum poṉṉamum tamiḻakattiṟ kiṭittup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. veṇṇiṟa māyiruppatu veḷḷi. veḷ-veḷḷi.

piṭikkappaṭṭu mutaṉmutalākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. cempu iyaṟkiyil taṉip poṉṉa niliyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv viyaṟkic cempu putuk kaṟkāla māntaṉ kalliṟkīṭākap payaṉ
paṭuttiya karuvipporu ḷākum. ataṉiṉṟu avaṉ muruṭṭuc cammaṭṭikaḷiyum kattikaḷiyum piṉṉar maṟṟak kalaṅkaḷiyum amittāṉ. ataṉ camaṭṭappaṭun taṉmiyāl, ati vēṇṭiya vaṭivattiṟku aṭittuk karuvikaḷi uruvākkuvataṟku mika eḷitā
yiruntatu. taṭṭuvataṉāṟ cempu iṟukik kūrmi mikkaṉavum nīṇṭuḻippaṉavu māṉa ōraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ap poṉṉattiṉ paḷapaḷappāṉa cempaṭṭi niṟamum niliyāṉa taṉmikaḷum ati mikunta vilimatippuḷḷatākkiṉa.
im muntu kālattiṟ cempiṟkākac ceyta tēṭuki, iyaṟkic cempik kaṇṭupiṭikkavum ataṉ vippukaḷiliruntu ati yeṭukkavum vaḻivakuttatu. ki.mu. 4000 āṇṭukaṭkuc caṟṟup piṉpu, ap poṉṉattik kuṭiyirupput tīkkaḷattil urukki vēṇṭiya vaṭivattil vārttukkoḷḷa muṭiyumeṉpatu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉpu, poṉṉaviyaṟ cempiṟkum cem puḷḷa pāṟikkumuḷḷa uṟavum, cempu kalanta maṇaliliruntu nerup piṉālum kariyiṉālum cempip piritteṭukka muṭiyumeṉpatum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉiṉṟu poṉṉavūḻi toṭaṅkip poṉṉakkali ciṟantatu. (poṉṉakkali varalāṟu pārkka).
cempuk kālat toṭakkattil ataṉ māperu vaḷarcci ekipatuvil iruntirukkak kūṭum. ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭuk kālattilēyē, kallaṟikaḷil iṟantavar payaṉpāṭṭiṟku vikkappaṭṭa paṭik kalaṅkaḷum karuvikaḷum cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.   ki. mu. 3800 pōl, cīṉāyt tīvakkuṟi   (Peninsula), ciṉeppuru (Snefru) aracaṉāl ceppuc curaṅkaṅkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭatippaṟṟiya tiṭṭa
māṉa eḻuttēṭukaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic curaṅkaṅ kaḷiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa kukikaḷ, ap poṉṉattip pirit teṭukkuṅ kali tūymippaṭuttaliyum uṭkoṇṭateṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ak kali, cempic caṉṉat takaṭukaḷāka aṭittu, avaṟṟik kuḻāykaḷākavum piṟa vuruppaṭikaḷākavum ceykiṟa aḷaviṟku vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntatu. akkālattil uṟi (veṇkalam) mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṟṟu. am mūlakkaruvip poruḷiṉ aṟiyappaṭṭa mutaṟ paḻantuṇṭu, metum (Medum) kūmpuk kōpurattiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkap
paṭṭuḷḷa oru veṇkalap pāriyākum. ataṉ tōṟṟakkālam tōrā. ki.mu. 3700 eṉṟu potuvāka oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
cempum veḷḷīyamuṅ kalanta aḷiyam (alloy) ākiya uṟi, cempiṉum uyarnta vaṉmi, viṟappu ākiya taṉmikaḷi uṭiyatu. itu potuvākap paṭikkalaṅkaḷum kaliyuruppaṭikaḷum ceyyuṅ karuvipporuḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. itu mikutiyākavum ciṟappākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kālam uṟikkālam (Bronze Age) eṉappaṭum. uṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttuki, ekipatuviliruntu kirēttāviṟkuk (Crete) ki.mu. 3000-lum, cicilikkuk (Sicily)

vākavum mikavuyarnta vilimatippuḷḷaṉavākavum iruntataṉāl, at tīviṉ āṭci, aṭuttaṭuttu ekipatiyarkkum acīriyarkkum pīṉiciyarkkum kirēkkarkkum pāracīkarkkum, urōmarkkum tāviṟṟu. urōmarkku vēṇṭiya cempu ēṟattāḻa muṟṟilum at tīviṉiṉṟē vantatu. ataṉāl atu ceppaṟip poṉmaṇal (aes cyprium) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ap poruḷuḷḷa ilattīṉap peyar kipriyam' eṉṟu kuṟukip piṉṉark kupram' eṉṟu tirintatu. ip peyariṉiṉṟē kāppar'  eṉṉum āṅkilac col vantatu. ilattīṉap peyariṉ mutalīreḻuttukaḷuñ cērntu ku' (cu) eṉṉum itaḷiyak (chemical) kuṟiyamikiṉṟatu.

veṇkalam tiṇmiyum vaṉmiyum uṭimiyiṉālēyē uṟiyeṉappaṭṭatu. muṟiyeṉpatum ap poruṭṭē. uṟattal = ceṟital. uṟutal = uṟutiyātal. uṟital=iṟukutal. muṟattal= viṟappātal. muṟuku=tiṇmi.
veḷḷi, poṉṉiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka aḻakuḷḷatum viliyuyarntatum aṇikalattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuvatumāṉa poṉṉa mākum. atu poṉpōl attuṇiyuyarnta taṉṟēṉum, ulakattiṟ kiṭikkumaḷavu mikak kuṟivākavē yuḷḷatu. ñālattiṉ kaṟpuṟaṇiyiṟ kiṭikkum arikkōṭip paṅku irumpiṟku, oru  paṅkutāṉ veḷḷi kiṭikkiṉṟatu.
veḷḷiyiṉ naṭimuṟip payaṉpāṭṭut toṭakkam, toṉ mikkuḷ mikat tolivu celkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum, māntaṉ mutaṉ mutaṟ paṇikkup payaṉpaṭuttiya poṉṉaṅkaḷ, poṉṉum cempumē yeṉṟu nampappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 4000 vari paḻimiyāṉa aracar kallaṟikaḷil, veḷḷi yaṇikalaṉkaḷum cuvaṭippukaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 3100 pōl ekipati āṇṭatākak karutappaṭum meṉeciṉ (Menes) neṟiyīṭṭut tokuppil, oru  paṅku poṉ, vili matippil iraṇṭarip paṅku veḷḷikkuc camameṉṟu tīrkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ituvē mutaṟ poṉṉaḷavit tiṭṭamāka irukkalām. ki.mu. 700ām āṇṭu, cintāṟṟiṟkum nīlāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ellā nāṭukaḷilum, poṉṉum veḷḷiyum paṇamāka vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatu perumpālum tēṟṟam. cnuhk®, j« fhy«tiu, btŸË¥ gÂ¡fiyÆY« m¿ÉaÈY« ntbwª eh£lhÇD« Äf¤ nj®¢á bg‰¿Uª âU¡fyh«.”-ãÇ¤jhÅa¡ kalik kaḷañciyam (1970). 20, pa. 536.
mēṟkāṭṭiya mēṟkōṭ pakutikaḷ, tamiḻaṟiyātavarāṟ perum pālum mēṉāṭukaḷēpaṟṟi yeḻutappaṭṭaviyātalāl, avaṟṟuḷ tamiḻar varalāṟṟiṟku ovvātaṉa vellām aṟiyāmiyiṉ viḷi veṉṟu karutiyamika.
iṟṟiyulakil mikap periya veḷḷiccuraṅkam mekcik kōvil (Mexico) uḷḷatu. amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷilum (U.S.A.) kāṉaṭāvilum veḷḷi peruvāriyākak kiṭikkiṉṟatu. teṉṉamerikkāvilum veḷḷi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutaṉmi yāṉa veḷḷi yīya nāka maṇalkaḷ āttirēliyāviṟ kiṭik kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟṟaḷavāka veḷḷi kiṭikkumiṭaṅkaḷ ulakeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, veḷḷik kalaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, veḷḷi vēynta māṭaṅkaḷum ampalaṅ
kaḷum araṇmaṉikaḷilum kōvilkaḷilum amintiruntaṉa.
		amiḻtaṉa marapi ṉūṉṟuvi yaṭicil 
		veḷḷi veṇkalat tūṭṭa laṉṟi	(puṟam. 390)
		viḷaṅkila vanti veḷḷi māṭat
		tiḷaṅkō vēṇmā ḷuṭaṉirun taruḷi	(cilap 25:4-5)
		veḷḷiyam palattu naḷḷiruṭ kiṭantēṉ 	(cilap. pati. 41)
veḷḷi ciṟṟaḷavākak kiṭittataṉāl, aracarālum peruñ celvarālum aṇivakip poruḷkaṭkaṉṟi, potu makkaḷāṟ palvakik karuvikaḷ ceyyap  payaṉpaṭuttappaṭavilli. ataṉāl, veḷḷikkālam eṉa oru kālamum ēṟpaṭavilli.
5. irumpukkālam (Iron Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000-ulaka muṭivu)
uṟikkup piṉ taṉip poṉṉamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatum, ulakeṅkum ērāḷamākak kiṭippatum, ellāk karuvikaḷuñ ceytaṟkēṟṟa uṟutimikkatum, nākarikamum aṟivum virintu vaḷartaṟ kētuvāyiruntatum, karumpoṉ eṉṉum irumpākum. ataṉ karumi niṟattāl irumpeṉappaṭṭatu. ir-iru-iruḷ. irumi = karumi. iru-irum-irumpu. E. iron. OE. iren.
acīriyarum ekipatiyarum irumpi mikutiyākap payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. tiruppottakam, paṭippiyal (ātiyākamam), 4ām atikāram 22ām tirumoḻiyil, tūpāl kāyīṉ irumpu cempu viṉiñarkkellām payiṟṟāḷaṉāka iruntāṉeṉṟu collap paṭṭuḷḷatu. tāvītiṉ kālattiṟ karuvikaḷum paṭik kalaṅkaḷuñ ceyya irumpu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ilakkiyak kirēkkariṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷāṉa tōriyap paṭiyeṭuppāḷar, iruppuk karuvikaḷiyum paṭikkalaṅkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. ākkileciṟku, avaṉatu valluṭam pāṇmip paricāka ōr iruppup pantu aḷikkappaṭṭa ceytiyi ōmar kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. kirēkkar karuṅkaṭaliṉ teṉkari yiṉiṉṟum, urōmar icuppāṉiyāvi ṉiṉṟum elpāviṉiṉṟum irumpip peṟṟaṉar. teḷivāka vuḷḷapaṭi, ataṟku muntiya mūlaṅkaḷ intiyāvil iruntaṉa. pirittāṉiyak kalikkaḷañciyam (1970), 12, pa.598              
tūpāl kāyīṉ kālam ki.mu.3874. ki.mu. 1725-l kāṉāṉiyar irupputtēr vittiruntaṉar. 1296-l kāṉāṉ aracaṉāṉa yāpīṉ 90 irupputtēr uṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. irumpu maṭṭumaṉṟit tērum mutaṉmutal tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ, ulakil mutaṉmutal tāṉē taṉiyākat tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāl, nīṇṭa kālamākac ciṟappup peyariṉṟi moḻi eṉṉum potup peyarālēyē vaḻaṅki vantatu. ōr ūril orē āṟiruppiṉ, āṟṟiṟkup pōy vantēṉ eṉṟē colvar. atupōl orē maramiruppiṉ, marattaṭikkup pō eṉṟē colvar. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻum tamiḻakattil orē moḻiyāyiruntataṉāl, moḻiyeṉṟē mutalil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
teṉṉila makkaḷ vaṭakkē ceṉṟapiṉ, taṭpaveppanili cuṟṟuc cārpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum,pēccuṟuppukaḷ vaṉmi peṟṟuviṭṭataṉālum, ilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅkaḷum pulavaru miṉmiyālum, vāykkicintavāṟu pēci vantataṉālum, avar  moḻi vallōciyum koccit taṉmiyum peṟṟut tiraviṭa niliyaṭintuviṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟa teṉṉila vaṇikar, vaṭanāṭṭil moḻipeyarntiruntatik kaṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl an nāṭṭi moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar. am moḻipeyar tēyat teṉṉelli varavarat teṟkē taḷḷi vantatu.
		kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu 
		valvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar 
		moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum	(kuṟun.11)
		villalit tuṇṇum vallāṇ vāḻkkit 
		tamiḻkeḻu mūvar kākkum 
		moḻipeyar tēetta paṉmali  yiṟantē.	(akam.31)
		paṉipaṭu cōli vēṅkaṭat tumpar 
		moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum	(akam.211)
		toṭiyami pakaḻit tuvaṉṟunili vaṭukar 
		piḻiyār makiḻar kaliciṟan tārkkum 
		moḻipeyar tēem iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum	(akam.295)
ivi piṟkālattaṉavāyiṉum, muṟkālattu nilimiyiyum oruvāṟu kuṟikkum.
nilavāṇikam pōṉṟē nīrvāṇikamuñ ciṟantatu. āṟṟik kaṭakkap parical, ampi, ōṭam, paḷḷiyōṭam mutaliya kala vakikaḷum; kālvāy āṟu kariyōrak kaṭal ākiyavaṟṟiṟ carakkukaḷik  kaṭattat  tōṇi, paḵṟi mutaliya kaṭattu vakikaḷum; kaṭaliluṭ ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭikkak kaṭṭumaram, mēṅkā, timil, paṭaku mutaliya ciṟukala vakikaḷum;muttuk kuḷikkac calaṅkup paṭakum; āḻkaṭalik kaṭantu akkari nāṭukaḷil vāṇikañceytu mīḷak kappal, nāvāy, vaṅkam mutaliya peruṅkala vakikaḷum  ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
neytal nilattil mīṉpiṭikkum toḻilarāyirunta paṭavar allatu paravar cilar, neṭuṅkaṭal cellum nīrvaṇikarāyiṉar. avaruḷ talivar kuṟunila maṉṉarumāyiṉar. avar kuṭippeyar paratar, paratavar eṉavun tirintatu.
paṭam=tuṇi, cīli, pāy. paṭam-paṭavu = pāy kaṭṭiya tōṇi. 	         paṭava tēṟi (tiruvāca. 43:3).
paṭavu - paṭaku. paṭavu - paṭavaṉ = paṭakōṭṭi. paṭavar maṭamakaḷir    	(tiruppō. can. piḷḷit. mut.4).
paṭavaṉ - paravaṉ = paṭakēṟi mīṉ piṭippavaṉ. mīṉpala paravaṉ 	         valikoṇarn tiṭṭaṉaṉ (tiruman. 2031).
paravaṉ - paratavaṉ = 1. mīṉ piṭippōṉ. mīṉvilip paratavar" (cilap. 5:25). tiṇṭimil vaṉparatavar (puṟam.24:4). 2. nīrvaṇikaṉ, vaṇikaṉ. (cilap 5:157, uri). 3. kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ. teṉparatavar miṭalcāya (puṟam. 378).
paratavaṉ-parataṉ=1. mīṉpiṭippōṉ. paṭartirip paratar muṉṟil (kamparā. kārkāla. 74). 2. kaṭalōṭi. paratar malinta payaṅkeḻu mānakar" (cilap. 2:2). 3 .vaṇikaṉ. parata kumararum (cilap. 5:158).
paratavar kaṭalōṭikaḷum (Mariners) cuṟṟuk kaṭalōṭikaḷumā yiruntataṉāl (Circumnavigators), vaṭaticic ceṉṟu vaṭapār muṉiyil ciṟcila vēḷikaḷil tōṉṟum vaṇṇavoḷiyik kaṇṭu, ataṟku vaṭavi yeṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar.
vaṭam - vaṭavi = vaṭatici neruppu.
	vaṭavik kaṉalip piḻinteṭuttu                 	(taṉippāṭal).
vaṭavaṉal = vaṭavi.
	akkaṭaliṉ mītu vaṭavaṉal niṟka viliyō	(tāyu. paripūra.9)
	veḷḷat tiṭivāḻ vaṭavaṉali 	(kamparā. tilamā. 86)
vaṭavaṉalam = vaṭavi
	kaṭukiya vaṭavaṉa lattiṭi vittatu	(kaliṅ. 402)
vaṭam - vaṭanti = vaṭaticiyiluḷḷatu, vaṭakāṟṟu.
	vaṭantittī  =  vaṭavi. cuṭarnteri vaṭantit tīyum
			(kāñcippu. irupat. 384).
uttara maṭaṅkal = vaṭavi (tivā.). uttaram = vaṭakku.
maṭaṅkal = kūṟṟuvaṉpōl ulakiyaḻikkum ūḻittī.
uttaram = vaṭakkiluḷḷa ūḻittī, vaṭavi (piṅ.).
pāriṉ teṉmuṉiyilum vaṭavi pōṉṟa oḷi tōṉṟu mēṉum, āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉamiyāl, atippaṟṟiya ilakkiyak kuṟippum iṟantupaṭṭatu.
vaṭavaṉal kumarināṭu muḻuku muṉṉarē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa tēṉum, atippaṟṟiya kuṟippuḷḷa iṟṟip paṇṭinūl 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa tivākaramē. eṉiṉum vaṭaviyi mutaṉ mutal kaṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē eṉpataṟku, atuvē pōtiya cāṉṟām. ēṉeṉiṉ, iṟṟi yaṟiviyalkaḷik kaṇṭa mēṉāṭṭārum ati 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē aṟintaṉar. kācanti (Gassendi) eṉṉum pireñciya aṟiviyalār 1621 -l atik kaṇṭu ataṟku vaṭaviṭiyal' (Aurora Borealis) eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu ap peyarkku vaṭavoḷi yeṉṟē poruḷ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu,. ataṉ viḷakkam:
“A luminous atmospheric phenomenon, now considered to be of electrical character, occurring in the vicinity of, or radiating from, the earth's northern or southern magnetic pole, and visible from time to time by night over more or less of the adjoining hemisphere, or even of the earth's surface generally;  popularly called the Northern (or Southern) LIghts.......” eṉṟu erutantuṟi āṅkilap pērakaramutaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻppeyarum ilattīṉap peyarum ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
nilavaṇikar vaṭakkiṟ paṉimalivariyum vaṭamēṟkil mēliyāciyāvariyum, nīrvaṇikar palticiyilumuḷḷa akkari nāṭukaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷi yellāṅ kēṭṭapiṉ, tam moḻi oṉṟilēyē ḻakaram ciṟappā yolittalik kaṇṭu, ataṟkut tamiḻ eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkalām.
tam-tami=1. taṉimi. taminiṉṟu (tirukkō. 167). 
2. oppiṉmi (caṅ. 81).
tami + ḻ = tamiḻ (taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭa moḻi). tamiḻ - tamiḻam.
tamiḻiṉ iṉimiyiyum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭiṉ cīrmiyiyum nōkki,
iṉimiyu nīrmiyun tamiḻeṉa lākum (10:580)
eṉṟār piṅkala muṉivar. av viraṇṭum vaḻipporuḷē yaṉṟit tamiḻ eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērpporu ḷākā.
collamippum, cemmarapum, ilakkaṇa voḻuṅkum, aivakit toṭi yaminta nālvakip pāviṉ  iṉṉōciyum, poruḷilakkaṇamum, ciṟappāka akapporuṭṭuṟikaḷum, iṉkati taḻuviya vaṉappiyalum, tamiḻiṉ iṉimiyiyum; poruṇmoḻip pākkaḷum araciyal nūlum aṟanūlum meypporuḷnūlum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭiṉ nērmiyiyum kāṭṭum.
akapporuṭ ṭuṟikaḷiṉ iṉimiyi nukarntē, māṇikka vācakar yāviyum pāṭiyapiṉ kōviyum pāṭi, atil,
		ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ					cintiyuḷḷum
		uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇṭīntamiḻiṉ 
	tuṟivāynuḻintaṉiyō.........	(tirukkō. 20)
eṉṟu, tam ciṟappāṉa īṭupāṭṭik kuṟippākat terivittār.
irumpukkālattil, veṇpā mutaliya nālvakip pākkaḷuḷ mutal iraṇṭiṉ kalappāl maruṭpā eṉṉum kalavip pāvum, kalippāviṉ tiripāl paripāṭal eṉṉum tiripāvum  tōṉṟiṉa. ici, nāṭakam, maruttuvam, camiyal, kaṇakku, kaṇiyam, ēraṇam, maṟi, meypporuḷ mutaliya paltuṟik kalikaḷum aṟiviyal kaḷumpaṟṟiya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷum;  ammi, aḻaku, toṉmi, tōl, viruntu, iyipu, pulaṉ, iḻipu eṉṉum eṇvaki vaṉappiyalum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. moḻiyuṭaṉ iciyum nāṭakamum iṇikkappaṭṭu, iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻ vaḻakkum ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. aintiṇic coṟkaḷum cērntu, tamiḻ peruvaḷam peṟṟiruntatu.
talikkaḻakam
muttamiḻum oruṅkē kaṟṟa pulavar üṟṟukkaṇakkiṉarntāṉṟiataṉāl,gḻiyaïlakkiyattiMrāyavumòâyiykkiyattiiaṟṟavum,pahṇṭiyaṉpa~ṟuḷiyahṟṟaṅkarikliruntamaJriaṉṉumta‹tilanakariloUkaHkamniWÉṉāṉ.ata‹ uṟuppiṉar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉṟum, avarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa mutunāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyā
viriyum pala paripāṭalum piṟavumeṉṟum, ak kaḻakatti naṭatti vanta pāṇṭiyar kāyciṉavaḻuti mutal kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāka eṇpat
toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruṭ pāvaraṅkēṟiṉār eḻuva
reṉṟum, ak kaḻakam irunta kāla nīṭci nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatāṇṭeṉṟum iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷ uriyiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ak kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉar peyarkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. akattiyar iṭikkaḻakak kuliviṟkuppiṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta āriyar. mutaliru kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarum tūya tamiḻarāvar. civaṉ peyarum murukaṉ peyaruṅ koṇṭa iruvar talikkaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarā yiruntirukkalām. murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar ki.mu. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭup pārata kālattavar. nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉpatu. vaṭaticit talivaṉik kuṟikkum tolkatik kaṭṭuppeyar.
talikkaḻakam ki. mu. 10,000 pōl tōṉṟiyatu. ak kālattu makkaḷ ikkālattāriṉum mika nīṇṭu vāḻntiruppa rātalāl, carācari āḷukku 50 āṇṭu vittukkoḷḷiṉ, 86 pāṇṭi yarukkum 4450 āṇṭākum. mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kāla nīṭci 4440 āṇṭu. itu muṟṟum poruttamāṉatum nikaḻntirukkak kūṭiyatumākum.
talikkaḻakattiṟku aḷavi nūlāyirunta māpiṇṭa nūṟ peyarum maṟintuviṭṭatu. akattiyam oru māpiṇṭamāyiṉum, iṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatātaliṉ, talikkaḻaka nūlāyirun tirukkamuṭiyātu. eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟ                            ṟilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa iyaṟṟamiḻ nūl piṇṭam; iyalici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa nūl māpiṇṭam. ovvoru tamiḻum ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum irupāṟ paṭṭatu. mut tamiḻkkum ilakkaṇam orē nūlāyirukkum;  āyiṉ, ilakkiyam vev vēṟu vaṉappukaḷākavum paṉuvalkaḷākavum irukkum. iyaṟṟamiḻilak kaṇap poruṭkūṟṟil yāppum uvami yeṉṉum aṇiyum aṭaṅkum.
talikkaḻakam varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmiyatātalāl, atippaṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉiyiṟ cila viḷattaṅkaḷ ki.pi.6ām allatu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil variyappaṭṭa vuriyil, tavaṟākak kuṟikkap               paṭṭiruppatu iyaṟkiyē.
talikkaḻakam toṭaṅkiyapiṉ, ulakiyaṟ kalikaḷum aṟi viyalkaḷum vaḷarntu vantatupōṉṟē, mataviyal ārāycciyum āḻntu vaḷarntatu. civaneṟikkum tirumāl neṟikkum mēṟpaṭṭa, kaṭavuḷ neṟiyeṉṉum potu neṟiyum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. potumakkaḷ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattiyum, pulamakkaḷ peruntēva matattiyum, tuṟaviyar kaṭavuḷ neṟiyiyum   kaṭippiṭittaṉar.
āyiṉ, kāḷi pōrveṟṟit teyvamākavum ammi nōyt teyvamākavuṅ karutappaṭṭataṉāl, nāḷaṭivil aintiṇip potut teyvamāṉāḷ. tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭumākiya teṉṉilattiṉ teṉkōṭiyilirunta perumalit toṭarukkum, vaṭakōṭiyilirunta pērāṟṟiṟkum, kumari yeṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarē iṭappeṟṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ matikkulattā ṉātalāl, avaṉ mutal talinakar avaṉ kulamutalvaṉ peyariyoṭṭi maturi yeṉappaṭṭatu. mati-matiri-maturi. o.nō; kuti - kutiri. kutittal = tāṇṭutal.
viki maturi piṟkālatta tātalāl, maruta muṉṟuṟi' eṉṉum toṭariṉiṉṟu maturip peyar vanta teṉpatu poruntātu. paḵṟuḷi maturip peyarē viki maturikkum piṟkālattil iṭappaṭṭatu.
talikkaḻakak kāla nākarikamum paṇpāṭum
āriyar teṉṉāṭu varukikku muṟpaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻakat taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷaṉittum aḻiyuṇṭu pōyiṉum, moḻiyamippi ṉiṉṟum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟum kaṭik
kaḻakap pāṭalkaḷilum paṉuvalkaḷilumuḷḷa kuṟippukaḷiṉiṉṟum, talikkaḻakak kāla nākarikap paṇpāṭṭi oruvāṟuṇaralām.
moḻi
aḵṟiṇiyiṉiṉṟu makkaḷip pirittuk kāṭṭuvatum, oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu aḷavukōlum moḻiyē. moḻiyiṉālēyē māntaṉ naraṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. 
naralutal = 1. olittal. āṭukaḻi naralum cēṭcimi (puṟam. 	       120). 2. kattutal.veṇkuruku narala vīcum nuṇpa ḵ ṟuvaliya	       (akam. 14). 3. pēcutal.
naral - naraṉ = māntaṉ. vaṟitē niliyāta vimmaṇ ṇulakiṉ naraṉāka vakuttaṉi (tēvā.934:2). naraṉ - naram = māntappiṟavi. narattilum piṟattināta (tiv. tiruccan.29).
vāṉaram (vālnaram)=vāliyuṭiya māntaṉ pōṉṟa vilaṅku. vāṉara mukaḷa (cīvaka. 1168).
naraṉ - va. nara. vāṉaram - va. vānara.
nara eṉṉuñ colliṟku vaṭamoḻiyil vērilli. vāṉara eṉṉuñ colli nara ēva' eṉṟu pirittu, naraṉip pōṉṟatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
moḻikku aṭuttapaṭiyāka, māntaṉiṉ ciṟappāṟṟal muṉṉutal. muṉṉutal=karututal. muṉṉuvāṉ (vāṉīṟṟu ni.kā.vi.e.) - muṉṉāṉ (ma.) AS. munan (to think).
muṉ-muṉṉam=1. karuttu.muṉṉa mukatti ṉuṇarntu (puṟam. 3). 2. kd«.(âth.). muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam = uḷḷam.
maṉam- t. maṉas. L. mens, ME. mynd, E. mind.
muṉ - maṉ = karutum āṟṟaluḷḷa māntaṉ.
maṉpatu - makkaṭ kūṭṭam, maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷivōṉ (paripā. 15:52).
maṉpatu - maṉpati = makkaṭkūṭṭam.
maṉpati kākkuniṉ purimi (puṟam. 210).
maṉ- t.kE. OS.,OHG. man, E man.
moḻiyiluṅ karuttilum aṉṟum iṉṟum eṉṟum ciṟantavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, moḻiyi vaḷarppavar potumakkaḷ; ilakkiyatti vaḷarppavar pulamakkaḷ. moḻikaḷ iyaṉmoḻiyum tirimoḻiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu iyaṉmoḻi;  oṉṟiṉiṉṟu tirintatu tirimoḻi. ākupeyarkaḷ irumaṭiyum mummaṭiyum ākuvatu pōṉṟu, tirimoḻikaḷum irumaṭiyum mummaṭiyun tiriyum. tiraviṭam tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tirimoḻi; āriyam tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu tirinta irumaṭit tirimoḻi.
kumarināṭu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam maṭṭumaṉṟi māntaṉ piṟantakamu mātalāl, tamiḻē immiyum ēṉimoḻi kalavāta muḻut tūya iyaṉmoḻiyākum.
irutiṇi aimpāl īreṇ mūviṭaṅkaḷākiya kiḷaviyilak kaṇamum, nuṇpirivukaḷiyuṭiya eṇ vēṟṟumip peya rilakkaṇamum, nannāṉku vakippaṭṭa mukkāla viṉi
yilakkaṇamum, vaḷami, cemmi, marapu, takuti, tūymi mutaliya coṟṟiṟaṅkaḷum; curukkam, teḷivu, matippuṟavu, iṭakkaraṭakkal, maṅkalam mutaliya coṟṟoṭaramitikaḷum koṇṭu; patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum patiṉāṟāriyap pirivukaṭku mūlamumā yamintatu tamiḻ.
irutiṇi
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum (uyiriyum) eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttālum, taṉakkum piṟarkkum nallatun tīyatumākiya iraṇṭiyum pakuttaṟiyum paṇpiyē ciṟappākak koṇṭu, aḵtuḷḷati uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum aḵtillatit tāḻnta vakuppeṉṟum tuṇintu, ataṟkēṟpa, uyarnta vakuppik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭkē āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālīṟum, tāḻnta vakuppik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭkellām orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum īreṇṇīṟumē koṭuttu, moḻiyi vaḷarttavar tamiḻap potumakkaḷē. makkaḷum tēvarum uyarnta vakuppu; maṟṟavi tāḻnta vakuppu.
e-ṭu :
orumi : murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi ceṉṟāḷ. - uyartiṇi.
kāḷi uḻukiṟatu, āvu kaṟakkiṉṟatu, kal kuttukiṟatu, atu paṟakkiṉṟatu. - aḵṟiṇi.
paṉmiyīṟum iruvakuppiṟkum vevvēṟām.
paṉmi : aracar vāḻntaṉar (vāḻntār), āciriyaṉmār pēciṉar.
- uyartiṇi.
kāḷikaḷ uḻukiṉṟaṉa, māṭukaḷ mēykiṉṟaṉa, avi iṉikkiṉṟaṉa, ilikaḷ acikiṉṟaṉa. - aḵṟiṇi.
makkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, avarkaḷ, vantārkaḷ eṉpaṉa vaḻuvamiti yākak koḷḷappeṟum.
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampal eṉṉum muṟiyil, potumakkaḷ vakutta vakuppukaṭkē ilakkaṇa nūlār uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉap peyar koṭuttaṉar. iṉṟum, kallā māntar ik kuṟiyīṭukaḷi aṟiyāviṭiṉum, irutiṇi muṟippaṭiyē pēcuvar. uyiriyum pāliyumē yaṉṟip pakuttaṟivi aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭu vēṟem moḻiyārum poruḷkaḷip pakuttilar. teluṅkil makat, amakat eṉṟatu tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟip piṟkālattilēyē.
vaḷami
	nālvakiyaṭi: vāḻiyili, neltāḷ, karumput tōki, paṉiyōli.
	palvakip piñcu: mā vaṭu, palā mūcu, vāḻik kaccal, teṉṉaṅ kurumpi, pākku nuḻāy.
kāyppu māṟiyapiṉ tōṉṟum piñcu nuru (noru).
yāṉippeyarkaḷ: āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kari, kaṟiyaṭi, kimmali, kimmā, tūṅkal, tumpi, tōl, nālvāy, pakaṭu, puḻikki-pūṭki, perumā, poṅkaṭi, matamā, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliyaṉa.
mukattiṟ cempuḷḷi yuḷḷatu cinturam allatu pukarmukam. murukaṉ ūrti piṇimukam. āṇyāṉi kaḷiṟu; peṇyāṉi piṭi.
ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta kutiriyiṉattiyum, pattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa vakiyākap pirittami muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻmoḻi, taṉakkuriya vīṭu eṉṉuñ colli maṭṭumaṉṟi, il eṉṉun teluṅkuc colliyum, maṉi yeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac colliyum, camaṟkirutattiṟkum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa kuṭi eṉṉuñ colliyum, taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷa teṉṟu, kālṭuvelār tamiḻccol vaḷattip pārāṭṭik kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.

oṉṟic collum vakiyippaṟṟimaṭṭum muppatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa viṉiccoṟkaḷ uḷa. tamiḻ varalāṟu' pārkka.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ ōrilakkam coṟkaḷik koṇṭiruntatu. an nāṭṭu muḻukkiṉālum, mutaliru kaḻaka vilakkiya aḻiviṉālum, pātic coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
cemmi
eḻuttukaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum, oliyum poruḷum tirikkātum citikkātum ilakkaṇa neṟiyiṟ pēcuvatu cemmi. cemmi tavaṟiyatu koṭumi. cemmiyāṉa tamiḻ centamiḻ. koṭumiyāṉa tamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ. tamiḻi eṉṟuñ centamiḻākavē vaḻaṅkavēṇṭu meṉpatu, toṉṉū lāciriyar iṭṭa niliyāṉa varampu. av varampiṉālēyē, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka āriyar ettuṇiyō kēṭu ceytirup piṉum, tamiḻ iṉṉum aḻiyātu iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
teluṅkil ṉakara vīṟṟi ṭakara vīṟākavum, kaṉṉaṭattiṟ pakara      mutali hakara mutalākavum, maliyāḷattil meliyaṭutta val leḻuttiyum meliyākavum vaṭārkkāṭṭu āmpūr vaṭṭattil ḻakaratti yakaramākavum olippatu olittiripāṉa koṭuntamiḻām.
ceruppu, tiruppu, neruppu, paruppu eṉpavaṟṟi, muṟiyē, ceppu, tippu, nippu, pappu eṉat teluṅkil tirippatu coṟṟiripāṉa koṭuntamiḻām.

viṭicollutal eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷuḷḷa cepputaṟ collic collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷil teluṅkil tirippatum, āyntu pārttal eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷuḷḷa nōṭutaṟ collip pārttal eṉṉum potup poruḷiṟ kaṉṉaṭattil tirippatum, putupperukku nīr eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷuḷḷa veḷḷam eṉṉum colli nīr eṉṉum potup poruḷil maliyāḷattil tirippatum, poruṭṭiripāṉa koṭuntamiḻām.

eṉpavaṟṟik kaṇṭum avar uṇarvatilli. potumakkaḷuḷ oru cārārāṉa  kīḻmakkaḷ  pēcciyun  taḻuvavēṇṭu  meṉiṉ,   avar oḻukkattiyum piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākum. itu uyarntōrk kuṭam pāṭaṉṟu. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkum ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭiruppiṉum,  iraṇṭum  centamiḻ  vaḻakkēyeṉṟum, cemmi tamiḻiṉ uyirnāṭip paṇpeṉṟum, tamiḻukku vaḻunili yāṉavi  tiraviṭa  moḻikaṭku   vaḻānili  yākumeṉṟum, ata ṉālēyē  avi  taṉimoḻikaḷāka  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.

e-ṭu: iḷami
māntaṉ makavu (kuḻavi, cēy, piḷḷi), āṭṭukkuṭṭi, māṭṭuk kaṉṟu, erumikkaṉṟu, nāykkuṭṭi (kuruḷi), pūṉikkuṭṭi (piḷḷi), kaḻutikkuṭṭi, kutirik kuṭṭi, oṭṭakak kaṉṟu, kīrippiḷḷi, nāvippiḷḷi, aṇiṟpiḷḷi, yāṉik kaṉṟu (kayantali, muṉi, kaḷavam, kuṭṭi), yāḷiyaṇaṅku, pulikkuruḷi, arimākkuruḷi, ōnāykkuruḷi, karaṭikkuṭṭi (kuruḷi), narikkuruḷi, kuraṅkukkuṭṭi (kuḻavi, pārppu, paṟaḻ, makavu), māṉkuṭṭi (kaṉṟu), kācaṟikkaru, verukuppiḷḷi, elikkuṭṭi (kuñcu), tēṭkuñcu, pāmpukkuṭṭi.

kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil pulavar palar marapum (idiom) takutiyum (propriety) pōṟṟāmiyāl, pala coṟkaḷ tam ciṟappup poruḷi iḻantuviṭṭaṉa.
iṉṟu, kuḷampi(kāppi)cāppiṭutal, curuṭṭuk kuṭittal, kocuvali, tēṭkaṭi eṉpaṉa marapuvaḻuvām. ivi kuḷampi kuṭittal, curuṭṭup piṭittal allatu pukittal, ulaṅku vali allatu nuḷampuvali, tēṭkoṭṭu eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. campaḷatti (salary) ūtiyam (profit, gin) eṉpatum vaḻuvām. ilakkaṇap pulami nirampātavar nūlāciriyarum pottaka āciriyarum āvataṉālum marapu keṭukiṉṟatu.
